  37:              then jsonb_typeof(value) = 'number' and (value #>> '{}')::numeric between 0 and 1
  38:              else true
  39:         end
  40:     )
  41: );

  92: -- 5) Onay: sahibi korunur (AC-S6). KiÅŸisel ilan ortak havuza taÅŸÄ±nmaz. --------
  93: create or replace function approve_raw_listing(p_raw_listing_id uuid)
  94: returns uuid
  95: language plpgsql
  96: security invoker
  97: set search_path = public
  98: as $$
  99: declare
 100:     v_parsed jsonb;
 101:     v_source_group_name text;
 102:     v_owner uuid;
 103:     v_listing_id uuid;
 104: begin
 105:     select parsed, source_group_name, owner_agent_id
 106:       into v_parsed, v_source_group_name, v_owner
 107:     from raw_listings
 108:     where id = p_raw_listing_id and status = 'pending';
 109: 
 110:     if v_parsed is null then
 111:         raise exception 'raw_listing bulunamadÄ± veya zaten incelenmiÅŸ: %', p_raw_listing_id;
 112:     end if;
 113: 
 114:     insert into listings (raw_listing_id, title, price, currency, rooms, area_m2, city, district, neighborhood, description, source_group_name, owner_agent_id)
 115:     values (
 116:         p_raw_listing_id,
 117:         coalesce(v_parsed->>'title', trim(concat(v_parsed->>'rooms', ' ', v_parsed->>'district', ' ', v_parsed->>'city'))),
 118:         (v_parsed->>'price')::numeric,
 119:         coalesce(v_parsed->>'currency', 'TRY'),
 120:         v_parsed->>'rooms',
 121:         (v_parsed->>'area_m2')::numeric,
 122:         v_parsed->>'city',
 123:         v_parsed->>'district',
 124:         v_parsed->>'neighborhood',
 125:         v_parsed->>'description',
 126:         v_source_group_name,
 127:         v_owner
 128:     )
 129:     returning id into v_listing_id;
 130: 
 131:     update raw_listings
 132:     set status = 'approved', reviewed_by = auth.uid(), reviewed_at = now()
 133:     where id = p_raw_listing_id;
 134: 
 135:     return v_listing_id;
 136: end;
 137: $$;

 139: -- 6) AkÄ±ÅŸ RPC'leri: gÃ¶rÃ¼nÃ¼rlÃ¼k = ortak (owner null) veya kendi (owner = auth.uid()).
 140: --    GÃ¼nlÃ¼k limit yalnÄ±z ortak ilanlarÄ± sayar; kiÅŸisel ilanlar kilitlenmez (AC-8).
 141: --    p_filters.scope = 'mine' -> yalnÄ±z kendi kiÅŸisel ilanlarÄ±.

 143: create or replace function feed_access()
 144: returns table (has_access boolean, lim integer)
 145: language plpgsql
 146: security definer
 147: set search_path = public
 148: as $$
 149: declare
 150:     v_plan_id uuid;
 151:     v_max integer;
 152: begin
 153:     select p.id, p.max_listings
 154:       into v_plan_id, v_max
 155:     from subscriptions s
 156:     left join plans p on p.id = s.plan_id
 157:     where s.agent_id = auth.uid()
 158:       and s.status in ('active', 'trialing', 'past_due')
 159:       and s.current_period_end > now()
 160:     order by s.created_at desc
 161:     limit 1;
 162: 
 163:     if not found then
 164:         return query select false, null::integer;
 165:         return;
 166:     end if;
 167: 
 168:     if v_plan_id is null then
 169:         raise log 'feed_plan_missing';
 170:         return query select true, 10;
 171:         return;
 172:     end if;
 173: 
 174:     if v_max is null then
 175:         return query select true, null::integer;
 176:         return;
 177:     end if;
 178: 
 179:     if v_max <= 0 then
 180:         raise log 'feed_limit_clamped';
 181:         return query select true, 0;
 182:         return;
 183:     end if;
 184: 
 185:     return query select true, v_max;
 186: end;
 187: $$;

 191: create or replace function feed_listings(p_filters jsonb default '{}'::jsonb, p_page int default 1)
 192: returns jsonb
 193: language plpgsql
 194: security definer
 195: set search_path = public
 196: as $$
 197: declare
 198:     v_has boolean;
 199:     v_lim integer;
 200:     v_filters jsonb := coalesce(p_filters, '{}'::jsonb);
 201:     v_page integer := greatest(1, least(coalesce(p_page, 1), 500));
 202:     v_il text := v_filters->>'il';
 203:     v_ilce text := v_filters->>'ilce';
 204:     v_grup text := v_filters->>'grup';
 205:     v_oda text := v_filters->>'oda';
 206:     v_scope text := v_filters->>'scope';
 207:     v_fmin numeric := case when v_filters->>'fiyatMin' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (v_filters->>'fiyatMin')::numeric end;
 208:     v_fmax numeric := case when v_filters->>'fiyatMax' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (v_filters->>'fiyatMax')::numeric end;
 209:     v_has_price boolean := v_fmin is not null or v_fmax is not null;
 210:     v_items jsonb;
 211:     v_locked bigint;
 212:     v_open_today bigint;
 213: begin
 214:     select a.has_access, a.lim into v_has, v_lim from feed_access() a;
 215: 
 216:     if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
 217:         return jsonb_build_object(
 218:             'items', '[]'::jsonb,
 219:             'locked_count', 0,
 220:             'limit', 0,
===== supabase/migrations/0024_wa_groups_personal_listings.sql lines 221-440 =====
 221:             'reason', 'no_subscription'
 222:         );
 223:     end if;
 224: 
 225:     with visible as (
 226:         select l.id, l.title, l.price, l.currency, l.rooms, l.area_m2,
 227:                l.city, l.district, l.source_group_name, l.created_at, l.owner_agent_id
 228:         from listings l
 229:         where l.owner_agent_id is null or l.owner_agent_id = auth.uid()
 230:     ),
 231:     ranked as (
 232:         -- SÄ±ra yalnÄ±z ortak ilanlar arasÄ±nda (partition owner null) -> kiÅŸisel ilan limite sayÄ±lmaz.
 233:         select
 234:             v.*,
 235:             row_number() over (
 236:                 partition by (v.owner_agent_id is null), (v.created_at at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date
 237:                 order by v.created_at desc, v.id desc
 238:             ) as rn
 239:         from visible v
 240:     ),
 241:     flagged as (
 242:         select
 243:             r.*,
 244:             (r.owner_agent_id is not null or v_lim is null or r.rn <= v_lim) as is_open,
 245:             ((r.created_at at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date
 246:                 = (now() at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date) as is_today
 247:         from ranked r
 248:     ),
 249:     filtered as (
 250:         select f.*
 251:         from flagged f
 252:         where (v_il is null or f.city = v_il)
 253:           and (v_ilce is null or f.district = v_ilce)
 254:           and (v_grup is null or f.source_group_name = v_grup)
 255:           and (not v_has_price or (f.currency = 'TRY' and f.price is not null))
 256:           and (v_fmin is null or f.price >= v_fmin)
 257:           and (v_fmax is null or f.price <= v_fmax)
 258:           and (
 259:                 v_oda is null
 260:              or (v_oda = '5+' and f.rooms in ('5+1', '5+2', '6+1', '6+2', '7+1', '8+1'))
 261:              or (v_oda <> '5+' and f.rooms = v_oda)
 262:           )
 263:           and (v_scope is distinct from 'mine' or f.owner_agent_id = auth.uid())
 264:     ),
 265:     page as (
 266:         select f.*
 267:         from filtered f
 268:         order by f.created_at desc, f.id desc
 269:         limit 20 offset (v_page - 1) * 20
 270:     )
 271:     select
 272:         coalesce(
 273:             jsonb_agg(
 274:                 case
 275:                     when p.is_open then jsonb_build_object(
 276:                         'id', p.id,
 277:                         'title', p.title,
 278:                         'price', p.price,
 279:                         'currency', p.currency,
 280:                         'rooms', p.rooms,
 281:                         'area_m2', p.area_m2,
 282:                         'city', p.city,
 283:                         'district', p.district,
 284:                         'source_group_name', p.source_group_name,
 285:                         'created_at', p.created_at,
 286:                         'personal', (p.owner_agent_id is not null),
 287:                         'owner_agent_id', p.owner_agent_id,
 288:                         'locked', false
 289:                     )
 290:                     else jsonb_build_object(
 291:                         'id', p.id,
 292:                         'city', p.city,
 293:                         'district', p.district,
 294:                         'created_at', p.created_at,
 295:                         'locked', true
 296:                     )
 297:                 end
 298:                 order by p.created_at desc, p.id desc
 299:             ),
 300:             '[]'::jsonb
 301:         ),
 302:         (select count(*) from filtered f2 where not f2.is_open),
 303:         (select count(*) from flagged g where g.is_today and g.is_open and g.owner_agent_id is null)
 304:     into v_items, v_locked, v_open_today
 305:     from page p;
 306: 
 307:     return jsonb_build_object(
 308:         'items', coalesce(v_items, '[]'::jsonb),
 309:         'locked_count', coalesce(v_locked, 0),
 310:         'open_today', coalesce(v_open_today, 0),
 311:         'limit', v_lim
 312:     );
 313: end;
 314: $$;
 315: 
 316: revoke all on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) from public;
 317: revoke execute on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) from anon;
 318: grant execute on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) to authenticated;

 320: create or replace function feed_filter_options(p_il text default null)
 321: returns jsonb
 322: language plpgsql
 323: security definer
 324: set search_path = public
 325: as $$
 326: declare
 327:     v_has boolean;
 328: begin
 329:     select a.has_access into v_has from feed_access() a;
 330: 
 331:     if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
 332:         return jsonb_build_object('cities', '[]'::jsonb, 'districts', '[]'::jsonb, 'groups', '[]'::jsonb);
 333:     end if;
 334: 
 335:     return jsonb_build_object(
 336:         'cities', (
 337:             select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
 338:             from (
 339:                 select distinct city as v from listings
 340:                 where city is not null
 341:                   and (owner_agent_id is null or owner_agent_id = auth.uid())
 342:             ) s
 343:         ),
 344:         'districts', (
 345:             select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
 346:             from (
 347:                 select distinct district as v from listings
 348:                 where district is not null
 349:                   and (p_il is null or city = p_il)
 350:                   and (owner_agent_id is null or owner_agent_id = auth.uid())
 351:             ) s
 352:         ),
 353:         'groups', (
 354:             select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
 355:             from (
 356:                 select distinct source_group_name as v from listings
 357:                 where source_group_name is not null
 358:                   and (owner_agent_id is null or owner_agent_id = auth.uid())
 359:             ) s
 360:         )
 361:     );
 362: end;
 363: $$;

 402: -- 7) RPC: emlakÃ§Ä± gruplarÄ± (AC-1, AC-10). YalnÄ±z kendi satÄ±rlarÄ±.
 403: create or replace function get_my_wa_groups()
 404: returns jsonb
 405: language plpgsql
 406: security definer
 407: set search_path = public
 408: as $$
 409: declare
 410:     v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
 411:     v_status text;
 412:     v_synced timestamptz;
 413:     v_groups jsonb;
 414: begin
 415:     if v_uid is null then
 416:         return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized');
 417:     end if;
 418: 
 419:     select c.status into v_status
 420:     from wa_connections c
 421:     where c.agent_id = v_uid;
 422: 
 423:     if not found then
 424:         return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'status', 'none', 'groups', '[]'::jsonb, 'max', 20);
 425:     end if;
 426: 
 427:     select max(g.updated_at) into v_synced
 428:     from wa_groups g
 429:     where g.agent_id = v_uid;
 430: 
 431:     select coalesce(jsonb_agg(
 432:         jsonb_build_object('jid', g.group_jid, 'name', g.name, 'selected', (s.group_jid is not null))
 433:         order by g.name nulls last, g.group_jid
 434:     ), '[]'::jsonb)
 435:     into v_groups
 436:     from wa_groups g
 437:     left join wa_group_selection s
 438:            on s.agent_id = g.agent_id and s.group_jid = g.group_jid
 439:     where g.agent_id = v_uid;
 440: 
===== supabase/migrations/0024_wa_groups_personal_listings.sql lines 441-660 =====
 441:     return jsonb_build_object(
 442:         'ok', true,
 443:         'status', v_status,
 444:         'groups', v_groups,
 445:         'max', 20
 446:     ) || case
 447:             when v_status = 'connected' then jsonb_build_object('groups_synced_at', v_synced)
 448:             else jsonb_build_object('readonly', true)
 449:          end;
 450: end;
 451: $$;

 457: -- 8) RPC: seÃ§imi tamamen deÄŸiÅŸtir (AC-2, AC-4, AC-S2, AC-S7, AC-S8). Tek transaction.
 458: create or replace function set_my_wa_group_selection(p_group_jids text[])
 459: returns jsonb
 460: language plpgsql
 461: security definer
 462: set search_path = public
 463: as $$
 464: declare
 465:     v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
 466:     v_status text;
 467:     v_jids text[];
 468:     v_matched integer;
 469:     v_added integer;
 470:     v_removed integer;
 471: begin
 472:     if v_uid is null then
 473:         return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized');
 474:     end if;
 475: 
 476:     if p_group_jids is null then
 477:         return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'invalid_input');
 478:     end if;
 479: 
 480:     if coalesce(cardinality(p_group_jids), 0) > 20 then
 481:         return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'too_many', 'max', 20);
 482:     end if;
 483: 
 484:     select c.status into v_status
 485:     from wa_connections c
 486:     where c.agent_id = v_uid;
 487: 
 488:     if v_status is distinct from 'connected' then
 489:         return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'not_connected');
 490:     end if;
 491: 
 492:     -- Eleman null ya da >200 karakter -> unknown_group (hiÃ§biri yazÄ±lmaz).
 493:     if exists (
 494:         select 1 from unnest(p_group_jids) j
 495:         where j is null or char_length(j) > 200
 496:     ) then
 497:         return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unknown_group');
 498:     end if;
 499: 
 500:     v_jids := array(select distinct j from unnest(p_group_jids) j);
 501: 
 502:     -- Her jid bu hesabÄ±n wa_groups kaydÄ±nda olmalÄ±; biri bile deÄŸilse hiÃ§biri yazÄ±lmaz.
 503:     select count(*) into v_matched
 504:     from wa_groups g
 505:     where g.agent_id = v_uid
 506:       and g.group_jid = any(v_jids);
 507: 
 508:     if v_matched <> coalesce(cardinality(v_jids), 0) then
 509:         return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unknown_group');
 510:     end if;
 511: 
 512:     select count(*) into v_added
 513:     from unnest(v_jids) j
 514:     where not exists (
 515:         select 1 from wa_group_selection s
 516:         where s.agent_id = v_uid and s.group_jid = j
 517:     );
 518: 
 519:     select count(*) into v_removed
 520:     from wa_group_selection s
 521:     where s.agent_id = v_uid
 522:       and not (s.group_jid = any(v_jids));
 523: 
 524:     delete from wa_group_selection where agent_id = v_uid;
 525: 
 526:     insert into wa_group_selection (agent_id, group_jid)
 527:     select v_uid, j from unnest(v_jids) j;
 528: 
 529:     -- AC-S8: iÃ§eriksiz kayÄ±t (yalnÄ±z sayÄ±lar).
 530:     if v_added + v_removed > 0 then
 531:         insert into wa_connection_events (agent_id, attempt_id, from_status, to_status, error_code, requested_action, detail)
 532:         values (v_uid, null, v_status, v_status, 'selection_changed', null,
 533:                 jsonb_build_object('added', v_added, 'removed', v_removed));
 534:     end if;
 535: 
 536:     return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'selected', coalesce(cardinality(v_jids), 0));
 537: end;
 538: $$;
 539: 
 540: revoke all on function set_my_wa_group_selection(text[]) from public;
 541: revoke execute on function set_my_wa_group_selection(text[]) from anon;
 542: grant execute on function set_my_wa_group_selection(text[]) to authenticated;

 544: -- 9) RPC: eÅŸik (AC-7, AC-S5). YalnÄ±z admin; deÄŸer 0-1.
 545: create or replace function admin_set_threshold(p_threshold numeric)
 546: returns jsonb
 547: language plpgsql
 548: security definer
 549: set search_path = public
 550: as $$
 551: declare
 552:     v_uid uuid := auth.uid();
 553: begin
 554:     if v_uid is null then
 555:         return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized');
 556:     end if;
 557: 
 558:     if not exists (select 1 from agents a where a.id = v_uid and a.is_admin) then
 559:         return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'forbidden');
 560:     end if;
 561: 
 562:     if p_threshold is null or p_threshold = 'NaN'::numeric or p_threshold < 0 or p_threshold > 1 then
 563:         return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'invalid_value');
 564:     end if;
 565: 
 566:     insert into app_settings (key, value, updated_by, updated_at)
 567:     values ('personal_auto_approve_threshold', to_jsonb(p_threshold), v_uid, now())
 568:     on conflict (key) do update
 569:     set value = excluded.value,
 570:         updated_by = excluded.updated_by,
 571:         updated_at = excluded.updated_at;
 572: 
 573:     return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'threshold', p_threshold);
 574: end;
 575: $$;
 576: 
 577: revoke all on function admin_set_threshold(numeric) from public;
 578: revoke execute on function admin_set_threshold(numeric) from anon;
 579: grant execute on function admin_set_threshold(numeric) to authenticated;

-- 3) approve_raw_listing: 0016 hali (owner yok)
create or replace function approve_raw_listing(p_raw_listing_id uuid)
returns uuid
language plpgsql
security invoker
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
    v_parsed jsonb;
    v_source_group_name text;
    v_listing_id uuid;
begin
    select parsed, source_group_name into v_parsed, v_source_group_name
    from raw_listings
    where id = p_raw_listing_id and status = 'pending';

    if v_parsed is null then
        raise exception 'raw_listing bulunamadÄ± veya zaten incelenmiÅŸ: %', p_raw_listing_id;
    end if;

    insert into listings (raw_listing_id, title, price, currency, rooms, area_m2, city, district, neighborhood, description, source_group_name)
    values (
        p_raw_listing_id,
        coalesce(v_parsed->>'title', trim(concat(v_parsed->>'rooms', ' ', v_parsed->>'district', ' ', v_parsed->>'city'))),
        (v_parsed->>'price')::numeric,
        coalesce(v_parsed->>'currency', 'TRY'),
        v_parsed->>'rooms',
        (v_parsed->>'area_m2')::numeric,
        v_parsed->>'city',
        v_parsed->>'district',
        v_parsed->>'neighborhood',
        v_parsed->>'description',
        v_source_group_name
    )
    returning id into v_listing_id;

    update raw_listings
    set status = 'approved', reviewed_by = auth.uid(), reviewed_at = now()
    where id = p_raw_listing_id;

    return v_listing_id;
end;
$$;

-- 4) Feed RPC'leri: 0020 hali (owner filtresi yok, scope yok).
create or replace function feed_listings(p_filters jsonb default '{}'::jsonb, p_page int default 1)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
    v_has boolean;
    v_lim integer;
    v_filters jsonb := coalesce(p_filters, '{}'::jsonb);
    v_page integer := greatest(1, least(coalesce(p_page, 1), 500));
    v_il text := v_filters->>'il';
    v_ilce text := v_filters->>'ilce';
    v_grup text := v_filters->>'grup';
    v_oda text := v_filters->>'oda';
    v_fmin numeric := case when v_filters->>'fiyatMin' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (v_filters->>'fiyatMin')::numeric end;
    v_fmax numeric := case when v_filters->>'fiyatMax' ~ '^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$' then (v_filters->>'fiyatMax')::numeric end;
    v_has_price boolean := v_fmin is not null or v_fmax is not null;
    v_items jsonb;
    v_locked bigint;
    v_open_today bigint;
begin
    select a.has_access, a.lim into v_has, v_lim from feed_access() a;

    if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
        return jsonb_build_object(
            'items', '[]'::jsonb,
            'locked_count', 0,
            'limit', 0,
            'reason', 'no_subscription'
        );
    end if;

    with ranked as (
        select
            l.id, l.title, l.price, l.currency, l.rooms, l.area_m2,
            l.city, l.district, l.source_group_name, l.created_at,
            row_number() over (
                partition by (l.created_at at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date
                order by l.created_at desc, l.id desc
            ) as rn
        from listings l
    ),
    flagged as (
        select
            r.*,
            (v_lim is null or r.rn <= v_lim) as is_open,
            ((r.created_at at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date
                = (now() at time zone 'Europe/Istanbul')::date) as is_today
        from ranked r
    ),
    filtered as (
        select f.*
        from flagged f
        where (v_il is null or f.city = v_il)
          and (v_ilce is null or f.district = v_ilce)
          and (v_grup is null or f.source_group_name = v_grup)
          and (not v_has_price or (f.currency = 'TRY' and f.price is not null))
          and (v_fmin is null or f.price >= v_fmin)
          and (v_fmax is null or f.price <= v_fmax)
          and (
                v_oda is null
             or (v_oda = '5+' and f.rooms in ('5+1', '5+2', '6+1', '6+2', '7+1', '8+1'))
             or (v_oda <> '5+' and f.rooms = v_oda)
          )
    ),
    page as (
        select f.*
        from filtered f
        order by f.created_at desc, f.id desc
        limit 20 offset (v_page - 1) * 20
    )
    select
        coalesce(
            jsonb_agg(
                case
                    when p.is_open then jsonb_build_object(
                        'id', p.id,
                        'title', p.title,
                        'price', p.price,
                        'currency', p.currency,
                        'rooms', p.rooms,
                        'area_m2', p.area_m2,
                        'city', p.city,
                        'district', p.district,
                        'source_group_name', p.source_group_name,
                        'created_at', p.created_at,
                        'locked', false
                    )
                    else jsonb_build_object(
                        'id', p.id,
                        'city', p.city,
                        'district', p.district,
                        'created_at', p.created_at,
                        'locked', true
                    )
                end
                order by p.created_at desc, p.id desc
            ),
            '[]'::jsonb
        ),
        (select count(*) from filtered f2 where not f2.is_open),
        (select count(*) from flagged g where g.is_today and g.is_open)
    into v_items, v_locked, v_open_today
    from page p;

    return jsonb_build_object(
        'items', coalesce(v_items, '[]'::jsonb),
        'locked_count', coalesce(v_locked, 0),
        'open_today', coalesce(v_open_today, 0),
        'limit', v_lim
    );
end;
$$;

revoke all on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) from public;
revoke execute on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) from anon;
grant execute on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) to authenticated;

create or replace function feed_filter_options(p_il text default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
    v_has boolean;
begin
    select a.has_access into v_has from feed_access() a;

    if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
        return jsonb_build_object('cities', '[]'::jsonb, 'districts', '[]'::jsonb, 'groups', '[]'::jsonb);
    end if;

    return jsonb_build_object(
        'cities', (
            select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
            from (select distinct city as v from listings where city is not null) s
        ),
        'districts', (
            select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
            from (
                select distinct district as v from listings
                where district is not null
                  and (p_il is null or city = p_il)
            ) s
        ),
        'groups', (
            select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
            from (select distinct source_group_name as v from listings where source_group_name is not null) s
        )
    );
end;
$$;

revoke all on function feed_filter_options(text) from public;
revoke execute on function feed_filter_options(text) from anon;
grant execute on function feed_filter_options(text) to authenticated;

create or replace function feed_unread_count(p_since timestamptz)
returns integer
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
    v_has boolean;
    v_count integer;
begin
    select a.has_access into v_has from feed_access() a;

    if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
        return 0;
    end if;

    select least(count(*), 100)::integer into v_count
    from listings
    where created_at > p_since;

    return v_count;
end;
$$;

revoke all on function feed_unread_count(timestamptz) from public;
revoke execute on function feed_unread_count(timestamptz) from anon;
grant execute on function feed_unread_count(timestamptz) to authenticated;

create policy listings_subscriber_select on listings
    for select using (
        exists (
            select 1 from subscriptions s
            where s.agent_id = auth.uid()
              and s.status in ('active', 'trialing')
              and s.current_period_end > now()
        )
    );

===== C:\Users\YUSUF ÇİNAR\OneDrive\Belgeler\Masaüstü\projelerim\emlak-platform\supabase\migrations\0020_feed_daily_limit.sql =====
-- 0020_feed_daily_limit.sql
-- EML-98 / EML-99 (faz2b-plan-limitleri): plan bazlÄ± gÃ¼nlÃ¼k ilan gÃ¶rÃ¼ntÃ¼leme limiti.
-- DoÄŸrudan listings okuma (listings_subscriber_select) kaldÄ±rÄ±lÄ±r; akÄ±ÅŸ ve sayaÃ§lar
-- security definer RPC'lerden okunur. Kimlik (auth.uid()) ve plan DB'den Ã§Ã¶zÃ¼lÃ¼r.
-- Idempotent: tekrar Ã§alÄ±ÅŸtÄ±rÄ±labilir.

-- 1) Abonelik + plan Ã§Ã¶zÃ¼mÃ¼ (yardÄ±mcÄ±). YalnÄ±z diÄŸer definer fonksiyonlar Ã§aÄŸÄ±rÄ±r; grant yok.
--    EriÅŸim: en yeni active/trialing abonelik ve sÃ¼resi dolmamÄ±ÅŸ dÃ¶nem.
--    Plan: kaydÄ±n plan_id'si. Plan bulunamazsa 10 (en dÃ¼ÅŸÃ¼k limit) + log.
--    max_listings null â†’ sÄ±nÄ±rsÄ±z (lim null). <= 0 â†’ 0 (tÃ¼m ilanlar kilitli) + log.
create or replace function feed_access()
returns table (has_access boolean, lim integer)
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
    v_plan_id uuid;
    v_max integer;
begin
    select p.id, p.max_listings
      into v_plan_id, v_max
    from subscriptions s
    left join plans p on p.id = s.plan_id
    where s.agent_id = auth.uid()
      and s.status in ('active', 'trialing', 'past_due')
      and s.current_period_end > now()
    order by s.created_at desc
    limit 1;

    if not found then
        return query select false, null::integer;
        return;
    end if;

    if v_plan_id is null then
        raise log 'feed_plan_missing';
        return query select true, 10;
        return;
    end if;

    if v_max is null then
        return query select true, null::integer;
        return;
    end if;

    if v_max <= 0 then
        raise log 'feed_limit_clamped';
        return query select true, 0;
        return;
    end if;

    return query select true, v_max;
end;
$$;

revoke all on function feed_access() from public, anon, authenticated;

-- 2) AkÄ±ÅŸ: filtreden Ã–NCE gÃ¼n baÅŸÄ±na N hesabÄ±; filtre aÃ§Ä±k/kilitli kÃ¼meyi deÄŸiÅŸtirmez (AC-2).

revoke execute on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) from anon;
grant execute on function feed_listings(jsonb, integer) to authenticated;

-- 3) Filtre seÃ§enekleri: yalnÄ±z il/ilÃ§e/grup adlarÄ± (ilan alanÄ± yok, AC-S7).
create or replace function feed_filter_options(p_il text default null)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
    v_has boolean;
begin
    select a.has_access into v_has from feed_access() a;

    if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
        return jsonb_build_object('cities', '[]'::jsonb, 'districts', '[]'::jsonb, 'groups', '[]'::jsonb);
    end if;

    return jsonb_build_object(
        'cities', (
            select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
            from (select distinct city as v from listings where city is not null) s
        ),
        'districts', (
            select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
            from (
                select distinct district as v from listings
                where district is not null
                  and (p_il is null or city = p_il)
            ) s
        ),
        'groups', (
            select coalesce(jsonb_agg(s.v order by s.v), '[]'::jsonb)
            from (select distinct source_group_name as v from listings where source_group_name is not null) s
        )
    );
end;
$$;

revoke all on function feed_filter_options(text) from public;
revoke execute on function feed_filter_options(text) from anon;
grant execute on function feed_filter_options(text) to authenticated;

-- 4) OkunmamÄ±ÅŸ sayÄ±sÄ±: zil kilitli ilanlarÄ± da sayar (AC-7), Ã¼st sÄ±nÄ±r 100.
create or replace function feed_unread_count(p_since timestamptz)
returns integer
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
    v_has boolean;
    v_count integer;
begin
    select a.has_access into v_has from feed_access() a;

    if not coalesce(v_has, false) then
        return 0;
    end if;

    select least(count(*), 100)::integer into v_count
    from listings
    where created_at > p_since;

    return v_count;
end;
$$;

revoke all on function feed_unread_count(timestamptz) from public;
revoke execute on function feed_unread_count(timestamptz) from anon;
grant execute on function feed_unread_count(timestamptz) to authenticated;

-- 5) DoÄŸrudan okuma kapanÄ±r. listings_admin_select korunur.
drop policy if exists listings_subscriber_select on listings;

===== C:\Users\YUSUF ÇİNAR\OneDrive\Belgeler\Masaüstü\projelerim\emlak-platform\supabase\migrations\0007_raw_listings_fingerprint_unique.sql =====
do $$
declare
  affected_count integer;
begin
  with duplicates as (
    select id,
           row_number() over (partition by fingerprint order by created_at asc) as rn
    from raw_listings
    where status <> 'rejected'
  )
  update raw_listings
  set status = 'rejected'
  where id in (select id from duplicates where rn > 1);

  get diagnostics affected_count = row_count;
  raise notice 'fingerprint-unique-constraint: % mÃ¼kerrer satÄ±r rejected durumuna Ã§ekildi (soft, audit)', affected_count;
end $$;

drop index if exists raw_listings_fingerprint_idx;
create unique index raw_listings_fingerprint_unique_idx
    on raw_listings (fingerprint)
    where status <> 'rejected';

   1: // EML-107 / AC-1, AC-5, AC-S4, AC-S7: KiÅŸisel oturumlar iÃ§in grup ve mesaj sÄ±nÄ±flandÄ±rma saf fonksiyonlarÄ±.
   2: // DÄ±ÅŸ baÄŸÄ±mlÄ±lÄ±k yok; manager.js bu fonksiyonlarÄ± kullanÄ±r.
   3: 
   4: const GROUP_SUFFIX = '@g.us';
   5: const MAX_JID_LENGTH = 200;
   6: const MAX_GROUPS = 500;
   7: 
   8: // SayaÃ§ sÃ¶zleÅŸmesi (DavranÄ±ÅŸ SÃ¶zleÅŸmesi "Sidecar mesaj iÅŸleme" satÄ±r 1-10 + AC-S7).
   9: // Python tarafÄ± (personal_*, threshold_fallback) bu anahtarlarla eÅŸleÅŸir.
  10: const COUNTER_KEYS = [
  11:   'personal_auto_approved',
  12:   'personal_queued',
  13:   'personal_queued_noscore',
  14:   'personal_not_listing',
  15:   'personal_duplicate',
  16:   'personal_write_error',
  17:   'personal_promote_error',
  18:   'threshold_fallback',
  19:   'skipped_unselected',
  20:   'skipped_dm',
  21:   'groups_truncated',
  22:   'webhook_post_failed',
  23:   'selection_read_error',
  24: ];
  25: 
  26: // YalnÄ±z grup JID'leri iÅŸlenir; DM ve grup-dÄ±ÅŸÄ± JID'ler (yayÄ±n vb.) 'skipped_dm' sayÄ±lÄ±r.
  27: // SÄ±nÄ±flandÄ±rma iÃ§erik, gÃ¶nderen numarasÄ± veya JID loglamaz (AC-S4).
  28: // isListing parametresi kabul edilir ama sÄ±nÄ±flandÄ±rmayÄ± etkilemez: metinsiz mesajlar manager'da elenir.
  29: function classifyIncoming({ remoteJid } = {}, selectedSet) {
  30:   if (typeof remoteJid !== 'string' || !remoteJid.endsWith(GROUP_SUFFIX)) return 'skipped_dm';
  31:   if (!selectedSet || !selectedSet.has(remoteJid)) return 'skipped_unselected';
  32:   return 'process';
  33: }
  34: 
  35: // Hesap baÅŸÄ±na grup Ã¼st sÄ±nÄ±rÄ±; fazlasÄ± yazÄ±lmaz, truncated=true dÃ¶ner (AC-S7).
  36: function capGroups(groups, limit = MAX_GROUPS) {
  37:   const list = Array.isArray(groups) ? groups : [];
  38:   return { groups: list.slice(0, limit), truncated: list.length > limit };
  39: }
  40: 
  41: function createCounters() {
  42:   const counters = {};
  43:   for (const key of COUNTER_KEYS) counters[key] = 0;
  44:   return counters;
  45: }
  46: 
  47: // SayacÄ± yerinde artÄ±rÄ±r; yalnÄ±z ilgili anahtar deÄŸiÅŸir.
  48: function bump(counters, key) {
  49:   counters[key] = (counters[key] || 0) + 1;
  50:   return counters;
  51: }
  52: 
  53: // Baileys groupFetchAllParticipating Ã¶ÄŸelerini wa_groups satÄ±rlarÄ±na Ã§evirir.
  54: // subject yoksa name null; 200 karakteri aÅŸan JID sessizce elenir.
  55: function toGroupRows(agentId, metadataList) {
  56:   if (!Array.isArray(metadataList)) return [];
  57:   const rows = [];
  58:   for (const g of metadataList) {
  59:     const jid = g && g.id;
  60:     if (typeof jid !== 'string' || jid.length === 0 || jid.length > MAX_JID_LENGTH) continue;
  61:     const name = typeof g.subject === 'string' && g.subject.length > 0 ? g.subject : null;
  62:     rows.push({ agent_id: agentId, group_jid: jid, name });
  63:   }
  64:   return rows;
  65: }
  66: 
  67: // Baileys mesaj gÃ¶vdesinden dÃ¼z metin (bridge.js extractText ile aynÄ± kurallar).
  68: function messageText(message) {
  69:   if (!message) return null;
  70:   const unwrapped =
  71:     message.ephemeralMessage?.message ||
  72:     message.viewOnceMessage?.message ||
  73:     message.viewOnceMessageV2?.message ||
  74:     message;
  75:   return (
  76:     unwrapped.conversation ||
  77:     unwrapped.extendedTextMessage?.text ||
  78:     unwrapped.imageMessage?.caption ||
  79:     unwrapped.videoMessage?.caption ||
  80:     null
  81:   );
  82: }
  83: 
  84: module.exports = {
  85:   classifyIncoming,

  88:   bump,

  92:   COUNTER_KEYS,
  93: };
===== scraper/sidecar/sessions/index.js =====
   1: // EML-105: KiÅŸisel oturum yÃ¶neticisinin gerÃ§ek (Baileys) baÄŸlantÄ±sÄ±.
   2: // bridge.js giriÅŸinden Ã§aÄŸrÄ±lÄ±r: WA_MULTI_SESSION !== '0' ise startSessions().
   3: // Ortak kÃ¶prÃ¼ (bridge.js) bu modÃ¼lden baÄŸÄ±msÄ±z Ã§alÄ±ÅŸÄ±r; burada hata olursa ortak kÃ¶prÃ¼ durmaz.
   4: const path = require('node:path');
   5: const pino = require('pino');
   6: const { default: makeWASocket } = require('@whiskeysockets/baileys');
   7: const { SessionManager } = require('./manager');
   8: const { SupabaseRest } = require('./supabase-rest');
   9: 
  10: const DEFAULT_MAX_SESSIONS = 50;
  11: const DEFAULT_WEBHOOK_URL = 'http://localhost:8090/baileys-webhook';
  12: 
  13: // EML-107: Sidecar Supabase istemcisine grup yazÄ±mÄ±/okuma. YalnÄ±z wa_groups upsert ve
  14: // wa_group_selection okuma; her sorgu agent_id ile sÄ±nÄ±rlÄ±.
  15: class SessionsRest extends SupabaseRest {
  16:   async upsertWaGroups(agentId, rows) {
  17:     this._agentFilter(agentId);
  18:     const updatedAt = new Date(this.now()).toISOString();
  19:     const body = rows.map((r) => {
  20:       if (r.agent_id !== agentId) throw new Error('grup satÄ±rÄ± agent uyuÅŸmuyor');
  21:       return { agent_id: r.agent_id, group_jid: r.group_jid, name: r.name, updated_at: updatedAt };
  22:     });
  23:     await this._request('POST', 'wa_groups?on_conflict=agent_id,group_jid', {
  24:       body,
  25:       prefer: 'resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal',
  26:     });
  27:   }
  28: 
  29:   async fetchSelection(agentId) {
  30:     const rows = await this._request('GET', `wa_group_selection?select=group_jid&${this._agentFilter(agentId)}`);
  31:     return (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []).map((r) => r.group_jid);
  32:   }
  33: }
  34: 
  35: function parseMaxSessions(raw) {
  36:   const n = Number.parseInt(raw || '', 10);
  37:   return Number.isInteger(n) && n > 0 ? n : DEFAULT_MAX_SESSIONS;
  38: }
  39: 
  40: // Baileys soketi: mesaj geÃ§miÅŸi ve Ã§evrimiÃ§i iÅŸaretleme kapalÄ±; messages.upsert dinleyicisi
  41: // bu fabrikada veya yÃ¶neticide EKLENMEZ (AC-4).
  42: function makeSocket(opts) {
  43:   return makeWASocket({
  44:     auth: opts.auth,
  45:     logger: pino({ level: 'silent' }),
  46:     printQRInTerminal: false,
  47:     syncFullHistory: false,
  48:     markOnlineOnConnect: false,
  49:     shouldSyncHistoryMessage: () => false,
  50:   });
  51: }
  52: 
  53: function startSessions(env = process.env) {
  54:   const url = env.SUPABASE_URL;
  55:   const serviceKey = env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
  56:   if (!url || !serviceKey) {
  57:     console.error('[wa] SUPABASE_URL/SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY yok; kiÅŸisel oturumlar baÅŸlatÄ±lmadÄ±');
  58:     return null;
  59:   }
  60: 
  61:   const webhookSecret = env.BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET;
  62:   if (!webhookSecret) {
  63:     console.error('[wa] BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET yok; seÃ§ili grup ilanlarÄ± iletilmez');
  64:   }
  65: 
  66:   const manager = new SessionManager({
  67:     rest: new SessionsRest({ url, serviceKey }),
  68:     makeSocket,
  69:     authRoot: env.WA_AUTH_ROOT || path.join(__dirname, '..', 'wa-auth'),
  70:     key: env.WA_AUTH_KEY,
  71:     maxSessions: parseMaxSessions(env.WA_MAX_SESSIONS),
  72:     webhook: { url: env.WEBHOOK_URL || DEFAULT_WEBHOOK_URL, secret: webhookSecret },
  73:   });
  74: 
  75:   manager.start().catch((e) => {
  76:     console.error('[wa] oturum yÃ¶neticisi baÅŸlatÄ±lamadÄ±', e && (e.name || 'error'));
  77:   });
  78:   return manager;
  79: }
  80: 
  81: module.exports = { startSessions, makeSocket };

   4: Baileys sidecar'Ä±n POST ettiÄŸi /baileys-webhook mesajlarÄ±nÄ± kuyruÄŸa alÄ±r,
   5: sabit boyutlu bir thread pool ile paralel iÅŸler: parse (Gemini) -> quality
   6: gate -> fingerprint dedup -> raw_listings'e yaz.
   7: 
   8: Mimari, maviLojistik/src/parsers/veri_cekici_ayristirici.py'deki
   9: queue.Queue + ThreadPoolExecutor + Ã¼Ã§ katmanlÄ± dedup desenine karÅŸÄ±lÄ±k gelir.
  10: """
  11: 
  12: import asyncio
  13: import hmac
  14: import json
  15: import logging
  16: import os
  17: import sys
  18: import threading
  19: from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
  20: from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
  21: from queue import Queue
  22: from typing import Optional, Set
  23: 
  24: from api_key_manager import get_default_manager
  25: from fingerprint import compute_fingerprint
  26: from parser import parse_listing_message
  27: from quality_gate import is_safe_to_auto_approve, score_listing
  28: from supabase_client import (
  29:     fingerprint_exists,
  30:     get_client,
  31:     get_threshold,
  32:     ingest_personal_listing,
  33:     insert_raw_listing,
  34: )
  35: 
  36: logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s")
  37: logger = logging.getLogger("scraper.orchestrator")
  38: 
  39: MAX_WORKERS = 10
  40: 
  41: message_queue: "Queue[dict]" = Queue()
  42: # AynÄ± mesaj ID'sinin kuyrukta iki kez iÅŸlenmesini Ã¶nleyen bellek-iÃ§i set
  43: # (DB'deki unique index son savunma hattÄ±, bu set ise anlÄ±k race-condition korumasÄ±).
  44: _active_ids_lock = threading.Lock()
  45: _active_ids: Set[str] = set()
  46: 
  47: 
  48: def _personal_outcome_key(status: str, score: Optional[float]) -> str:
  49:     """KiÅŸisel yazÄ±m sonucunu sidecar sayaÃ§ anahtarÄ±na Ã§evirir (DavranÄ±ÅŸ SÃ¶zleÅŸmesi satÄ±r 1-3, 7)."""
  50:     if status == "approved":
  51:         return "personal_auto_approved"
  52:     if status == "duplicate":
  53:         return "personal_duplicate"
  54:     if score is None:
  55:         return "personal_queued_noscore"
  56:     return "personal_queued"
  57: 
  58: 
  59: def _log_personal_outcome(key: str, agent_id: str) -> str:
  60:     """SayaÃ§ anahtarÄ±nÄ± iÃ§eriksiz tek satÄ±r olarak loglar (mesaj metni/numara/jid yok)."""
  61:     logger.info("personal_outcome=%s agent=%s", key, agent_id)
  62:     return key
  63: 
  64: 
  65: def _handle_personal(
  66:     client,
  67:     agent_id: str,
  68:     payload: dict,
  69:     message_id: str,
  70:     text: str,
  71:     parsed: dict,
  72: ) -> str:
  73:     """KiÅŸisel ilan yolu: owner yalnÄ±z agent_id'den gelir (AC-S3). Ortak insert/dedup'a girmez.
  74: 
  75:     GÃ¶nderen numarasÄ± record'a yazÄ±lmaz (AC-S4). EÅŸik: quality_score >= eÅŸik ise approved,
  76:     aksi halde pending (admin kuyruÄŸu). Yazma hatasÄ± yutulur; fallback yapÄ±lmaz (kÄ±smi yazÄ±m yok).
  77:     """
  78:     score, _issues = score_listing(parsed)
  79:     threshold, fallback = get_threshold(client)
  80:     if fallback:
  81:         _log_personal_outcome("threshold_fallback", agent_id)
  82:     status = "approved" if score is not None and score >= threshold else "pending"
  83:     record = {
  84:         "source": "whatsapp",
  85:         "source_group": payload.get("group"),
  86:         "source_group_name": payload.get("groupName"),
  87:         "source_message_id": message_id or None,
  88:         "raw_text": text,
  89:         "parsed": parsed,
  90:         "fingerprint": compute_fingerprint(parsed),
  91:         "quality_score": score,
  92:         "status": status,
  93:     }
  94:     try:
  95:         outcome = ingest_personal_listing(client, agent_id, record)
  96:     except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - iÃ§eriksiz logla, worker'Ä± dÃ¼ÅŸÃ¼rme
  97:         logger.exception("KiÅŸisel ilan yazÄ±lamadÄ± (iÃ§erik loglanmaz)")
  98:         return _log_personal_outcome("personal_write_error", agent_id)
  99: 
 100:     return _log_personal_outcome(_personal_outcome_key(outcome, score), agent_id)
 101: 
 102: 
 103: def handle_message(payload: dict) -> Optional[str]:
 104:     message_id = str(payload.get("id") or "")
 105:     text = (payload.get("text") or "").strip()
 106:     group = payload.get("group")
 107:     agent_id = payload.get("agentId")
 108: 
 109:     if not text:
 110:         return None
 111: 
 112:     try:
 113:         client = get_client()
 114:         key_manager = get_default_manager()
 115: 
 116:         if agent_id:
 117:             try:
 118:                 parsed = asyncio.run(parse_listing_message(text, key_manager))
 119:             except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - iÃ§erik loglanmaz, yalnÄ±z sayaÃ§
 120:                 return _log_personal_outcome("personal_parse_error", agent_id)
 121:             if parsed is None:
 122:                 return _log_personal_outcome("personal_not_listing", agent_id)
 123:             return _handle_personal(client, agent_id, payload, message_id, text, parsed)
 124: 
 125:         parsed = asyncio.run(parse_listing_message(text, key_manager))
 126: 
 127:         if parsed is None:
 128:             logger.info("Mesaj ilan deÄŸil veya parse edilemedi, atlandÄ±: %s", message_id)
 129:             return None
 130: 
 131:         fingerprint = compute_fingerprint(parsed)
 132:         if fingerprint_exists(client, fingerprint):
 133:             logger.info("MÃ¼kerrer ilan (fingerprint eÅŸleÅŸti), atlandÄ±: %s", message_id)
 134:             return None
 135: 
 136:         score, issues = score_listing(parsed)
 137:         record = {
 138:             "source": "whatsapp",
 139:             "source_group": group,
 140:             "source_group_name": payload.get("groupName"),
 141:             "source_message_id": message_id or None,
 142:             "raw_text": text,
 143:             "parsed": parsed,
 144:             "fingerprint": fingerprint,
 145:             "quality_score": score,
 146:             "status": "pending",
 147:             "owner_agent_id": None,
 148:         }
 149:         _inserted_row, inserted = insert_raw_listing(client, record)
 150:         if inserted:
 151:             logger.info(
 152:                 "raw_listings'e yazÄ±ldÄ±: %s (skor=%.2f, auto_approve_uygun=%s, sorunlar=%s)",
 153:                 message_id,
 154:                 score,
 155:                 is_safe_to_auto_approve(score, float(os.getenv("QUALITY_GATE_THRESHOLD", "0.5"))),
 156:                 issues,
 157:             )
 158:         else:
 159:             logger.info(
 160:                 "MÃ¼kerrer ilan (fingerprint Ã§akÄ±ÅŸmasÄ±, DB seviyesinde atlandÄ±): %s",
 161:                 message_id,
 162:             )
 163:     except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - worker thread hatayÄ± yutmasÄ±n, logla ve devam et
 164:         logger.exception("Mesaj iÅŸlenirken hata: %s", message_id)
 165:     finally:
 166:         with _active_ids_lock:
 167:             _active_ids.discard(message_id)
 168:     return None
 169: 
 170: 
 171: def _background_worker_loop(executor: ThreadPoolExecutor) -> None:
 172:     while True:
 173:         payload = message_queue.get()
 174:         executor.submit(handle_message, payload)
 175: 
 176: 
 177: class WebhookHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
 178:     def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802 - http.server API ismi
 179:         if self.path != "/baileys-webhook":
 180:             self.send_response(404)
 181:             self.end_headers()
 182:             return
 183: 
 184:         secret = os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET", "")
 185:         auth_header = self.headers.get("Authorization", "")
 186:         token = auth_header[len("Bearer "):] if auth_header.startswith("Bearer ") else ""
 187:         if not secret or not hmac.compare_digest(token, secret):
 188:             body = json.dumps({"error": "unauthorized"}).encode("utf-8")
 189:             self.send_response(401)
 190:             self.send_header("WWW-Authenticate", "Bearer")
 191:             self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
 192:             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
 193:             self.end_headers()
 194:             self.wfile.write(body)
 195:             return
 196: 
 197:         length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
 198:         body = self.rfile.read(length) if length else b""
 199:         try:
 200:             payload = json.loads(body or b"{}")
 201:         except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError):
 202:             # Body iÃ§eriÄŸi loglanmaz (PII riski).
 203:             logger.warning("GeÃ§ersiz webhook body (JSON/UTF-8 Ã§Ã¶zÃ¼lemedi), 400 dÃ¶nÃ¼ldÃ¼.")
 204:             response = json.dumps({"error": "invalid_body"}).encode("utf-8")
 205:             self.send_response(400)
 206:             self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
 207:             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response)))
 208:             self.end_headers()
 209:             self.wfile.write(response)
 210:             return
 211: 
 212:         if not payload.get("text"):
 213:             # Metinsiz mesaj kuyruÄŸa alÄ±nmaz; 200 ile "iÅŸlendi" denir (WhatsApp tekrar gÃ¶ndermesin).
 214:             response = b"{}"
 215:             self.send_response(200)
 216:             self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
 217:             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(response)))
 218:             self.end_headers()
 219:             self.wfile.write(response)
 220:             return
 221: 
 222:         message_id = str(payload.get("id") or "")
 223:         with _active_ids_lock:
 224:             if message_id and message_id in _active_ids:
 225:                 self.send_response(200)
 226:                 self.end_headers()
 227:                 return
 228:             if message_id:
 229:                 _active_ids.add(message_id)
 230: 
 231:         message_queue.put(payload)
 232: 
 233:         self.send_response(200)
 234:         self.end_headers()
 235: 
 236:     def log_message(self, format: str, *args) -> None:  # noqa: A002 - http.server imzasÄ±
 237:         logger.debug("%s - %s", self.address_string(), format % args)
 238: 
 239: 
 240: def run() -> None:
 241:     if not os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET", ""):
 242:         logger.error("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_SECRET env deÄŸiÅŸkeni set edilmemiÅŸ, sunucu baÅŸlatÄ±lmÄ±yor.")
 243:         sys.exit(1)
 244: 
 245:     host = os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_HOST", "127.0.0.1")
 246:     port = int(os.getenv("BAILEYS_WEBHOOK_PORT", "8090"))
 247:     executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=MAX_WORKERS)
 248: 
 249:     worker_thread = threading.Thread(target=_background_worker_loop, args=(executor,), daemon=True)
 250:     worker_thread.start()
 251: 
 252:     server = HTTPServer((host, port), WebhookHandler)
 253:     logger.info("Scraper worker %s:%s/baileys-webhook adresinde dinliyor.", host, port)
 254:     server.serve_forever()
 255: 
 256: 
 257: if __name__ == "__main__":
 258:     run()
===== scraper/supabase_client.py =====
   1: # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
   2: """Scraper worker'Ä±n Supabase'e yazÄ±mÄ± iÃ§in ince istemci.
   3: 
   4: Service-role key kullanÄ±r (RLS'i bypass eder) â€” bu dosya SADECE scraper
   5: worker'Ä±n kendi sunucusunda/container'Ä±nda Ã§alÄ±ÅŸÄ±r, tarayÄ±cÄ±ya asla gÃ¶nderilmez.
   6: """
   7: 
   8: import logging
   9: import os
  10: from typing import Any, Dict, Optional, Tuple
  11: 
  12: from postgrest.exceptions import APIError
  13: from supabase import Client, create_client
  14: 
  15: logger = logging.getLogger("scraper.supabase_client")
  16: 
  17: UNIQUE_VIOLATION_CODE = "23505"
  18: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD = 0.70
  19: THRESHOLD_KEY = "personal_auto_approve_threshold"
  20: PERSONAL_INGEST_STATUSES = ("approved", "pending", "duplicate")
  21: 
  22: 
  23: def get_client() -> Client:
  24:     url = os.environ["SUPABASE_URL"]
  25:     key = os.environ["SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY"]
  26:     return create_client(url, key)
  27: 
  28: 
  29: def insert_raw_listing(client: Client, record: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[Optional[Dict[str, Any]], bool]:
  30:     """raw_listings tablosuna tek kayÄ±t ekler (atomik, unique-violation'a karÅŸÄ± gÃ¼venli).
  31: 
  32:     record alanlarÄ±: source, source_group, source_message_id, raw_text,
  33:     parsed (dict), fingerprint, quality_score.
  34:     source_message_id Ã¼zerindeki unique index aynÄ± mesajÄ±n iki kez
  35:     eklenmesini veritabanÄ± seviyesinde engeller (dedup'Ä±n son savunma hattÄ±).
  36:     fingerprint kolonunda da (status <> 'rejected' koÅŸuluyla) PARTIAL bir
  37:     unique index var (bkz. migration 0007) â€” paralel worker'larÄ±n aynÄ±
  38:     fingerprint'i neredeyse aynÄ± anda insert etmeye Ã§alÄ±ÅŸtÄ±ÄŸÄ± race condition'Ä±
  39:     DB seviyesinde engeller. Bu fonksiyon, o unique-violation'Ä± (23505)
  40:     try/except ile yakalayÄ±p sessizce `(None, False)` dÃ¶ndÃ¼rerek atomikliÄŸi
  41:     saÄŸlar; 23505 DIÅINDA bir hata (gerÃ§ek DB/baÄŸlantÄ±/yetki hatasÄ±) YUKARI
  42:     FIRLATILIR, yutulmaz.
  43: 
  44:     DÃ¶nÃ¼ÅŸ: (kayÄ±t veya None, inserted: bool).
  45:     """
  46:     try:
  47:         result = client.table("raw_listings").insert(record).execute()
  48:     except APIError as error:
  49:         if error.code == UNIQUE_VIOLATION_CODE:
  50:             return None, False
  51:         raise
  52:     return (result.data[0] if result.data else {}), True
  53: 
  54: 
  55: def fingerprint_exists(client: Client, fingerprint: str) -> bool:
  56:     """Ortak havuz (owner_agent_id null) iÃ§in dedup. KiÅŸisel kayÄ±tlar bu kontrolÃ¼ tetiklemez (AC-11)."""
  57:     result = (
  58:         client.table("raw_listings")
  59:         .select("id")
  60:         .eq("fingerprint", fingerprint)
  61:         .is_("owner_agent_id", "null")
  62:         .limit(1)
  63:         .execute()
  64:     )
  65:     return bool(result.data)
  66: 
  67: 
  68: def get_threshold(client: Client) -> Tuple[float, bool]:
  69:     """app_settings'ten otomatik onay eÅŸiÄŸini okur.
  70: 
  71:     DÃ¶nÃ¼ÅŸ: (eÅŸik, fallback). Okuma hatasÄ±, eksik satÄ±r veya 0-1 dÄ±ÅŸÄ±/sayÄ± olmayan
  72:     deÄŸerde (0.70, True) dÃ¶ner: eÅŸik belirsizse ilan admin kuyruÄŸunda kalÄ±r.
  73:     """
  74:     try:
  75:         result = (
  76:             client.table("app_settings")
  77:             .select("value")
  78:             .eq("key", THRESHOLD_KEY)
  79:             .limit(1)
  80:             .execute()
  81:         )
  82:         rows = result.data
  83:     except Exception as error:  # noqa: BLE001 - eÅŸik okunamazsa varsayÄ±lana dÃ¼ÅŸ, akÄ±ÅŸ durmaz
  84:         logger.warning("EÅŸik okunamadÄ±, varsayÄ±lan kullanÄ±lÄ±yor: %s", type(error).__name__)
  85:         return DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, True
  86: 
  87:     if not isinstance(rows, list) or not rows:
  88:         logger.warning("EÅŸik ayarÄ± bulunamadÄ±, varsayÄ±lan kullanÄ±lÄ±yor")
  89:         return DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, True
  90: 
  91:     value = rows[0].get("value") if isinstance(rows[0], dict) else None
  92:     if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)) or not 0.0 <= value <= 1.0:
  93:         logger.warning("GeÃ§ersiz eÅŸik deÄŸeri, varsayÄ±lan kullanÄ±lÄ±yor")
  94:         return DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, True
  95:     return float(value), False
  96: 
  97: 
  98: def ingest_personal_listing(client: Client, agent_id: str, record: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
  99:     """KiÅŸisel ilanÄ± tek transaction'da yazar (service-role RPC).
 100: 
 101:     owner yalnÄ±z agent_id'den gelir; record iÃ§indeki owner alanÄ± RPC'ye taÅŸÄ±nmaz.
 102:     DÃ¶nÃ¼ÅŸ: "approved" | "pending" | "duplicate". Beklenmeyen dÃ¶nÃ¼ÅŸ ValueError fÄ±rlatÄ±r.
 103:     """
 104:     result = client.rpc(
 105:         "ingest_personal_listing",
 106:         {"p_agent_id": agent_id, "p_record": record},
 107:     ).execute()
 108:     status = result.data
 109:     if status not in PERSONAL_INGEST_STATUSES:
 110:         raise ValueError("ingest_personal_listing beklenmeyen sonuÃ§ dÃ¶ndÃ¼rdÃ¼")
 111:     return status
===== web/src/lib/push/targets.ts =====
   1: import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
   2: 
   3: /**
   4:  * Cron gÃ¶nderim hedefleri ve yardÄ±mcÄ± sorgular. YalnÄ±z service_role istemcisiyle
   5:  * Ã§aÄŸrÄ±lÄ±r (cron route). Ä°stemci girdisi almaz.
   6:  */
   7: 
   8: /** EriÅŸimi olan abonelik durumlarÄ± (AC-3). */
   9: export const ACCESS_STATUSES = ["active", "trialing", "past_due"] as const;
  10: 
  11: const PAGE_SIZE = 1000;
  12: 
  13: export type PushTarget = {
  14:   id: string;
  15:   agent_id: string;
  16:   endpoint: string;
  17:   p256dh: string;
  18:   auth: string;
  19:   /** Yeni ilan sayÄ±mÄ±nÄ±n baÅŸlangÄ±cÄ±: coalesce(last_notified_at, created_at). */
  20:   since: string;
  21: };
  22: 
  23: type PushRow = {
  24:   id: string;
  25:   agent_id: string;
  26:   endpoint: string;
  27:   p256dh: string;
  28:   auth: string;
  29:   created_at: string;
  30:   last_notified_at: string | null;
  31: };
  32: 
  33: type PageResult<T> = { data: T[] | null; error: unknown };
  34: 
  35: /**
  36:  * PostgREST varsayÄ±lan olarak 1000 satÄ±r dÃ¶ndÃ¼rÃ¼r; tÃ¼m sayfalarÄ± sÄ±rayla toplar.
  37:  * `order("id")` ile sayfalar kararlÄ± tutulur.
  38:  */
  39: async function selectAll<T>(
  40:   run: (from: number, to: number) => PromiseLike<PageResult<T>>,
  41: ): Promise<PageResult<T>> {
  42:   const out: T[] = [];
  43:   for (let from = 0; ; from += PAGE_SIZE) {
  44:     const { data, error } = await run(from, from + PAGE_SIZE - 1);
  45:     if (error) return { data: null, error };
  46:     const page = data ?? [];
  47:     out.push(...page);
  48:     if (page.length < PAGE_SIZE) return { data: out, error: null };
  49:   }
  50: }
  51: 
  52: /**
  53:  * EriÅŸimi olan (subscriptions: active/trialing/past_due ve current_period_end > now)
  54:  * agent'larÄ±n push abonelik satÄ±rlarÄ±nÄ± dÃ¶ner. EriÅŸimi olmayan satÄ±rlar silinmez,
  55:  * yalnÄ±z burada atlanÄ±r (AC-3).
  56:  */
  57: export async function loadTargets(
  58:   admin: SupabaseClient,
  59: ): Promise<{ data: PushTarget[] | null; error: unknown }> {
  60:   const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();
  61: 
  62:   const subs = await selectAll<{ id: string; agent_id: string }>((from, to) =>
  63:     admin
  64:       .from("subscriptions")
  65:       .select("id, agent_id")
  66:       .in("status", [...ACCESS_STATUSES])
  67:       .gt("current_period_end", nowIso)
  68:       .order("id")
  69:       .range(from, to),
  70:   );
  71:   if (subs.error) return { data: null, error: subs.error };
  72: 
  73:   const accessible = new Set((subs.data ?? []).map((s) => s.agent_id));
  74:   if (accessible.size === 0) return { data: [], error: null };
  75: 
  76:   const rows = await selectAll<PushRow>((from, to) =>
  77:     admin
  78:       .from("push_subscriptions")
  79:       .select("id, agent_id, endpoint, p256dh, auth, created_at, last_notified_at")
  80:       .order("id")
  81:       .range(from, to),
  82:   );
  83:   if (rows.error) return { data: null, error: rows.error };
  84: 
  85:   const data: PushTarget[] = (rows.data ?? [])
  86:     .filter((r) => accessible.has(r.agent_id))
  87:     .map((r) => ({
  88:       id: r.id,
  89:       agent_id: r.agent_id,
  90:       endpoint: r.endpoint,
  91:       p256dh: r.p256dh,
  92:       auth: r.auth,
  93:       since: r.last_notified_at ?? r.created_at,
  94:     }));
  95: 
  96:   return { data, error: null };
  97: }
  98: 
  99: const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
 100: 
 101: /**
 102:  * `since` anÄ±ndan sonra onaylanmÄ±ÅŸ ilan sayÄ±sÄ±, hedef agent'a gÃ¶rÃ¼nÃ¼r olanlar:
 103:  * ortak havuz (owner_agent_id is null) ve agent'Ä±n KENDÄ° kiÅŸisel ilanlarÄ± (AC-S1).
 104:  * BaÅŸka emlakÃ§Ä±nÄ±n kiÅŸisel ilanÄ± bu agent'a sayÄ±lmaz.
 105:  */
 106: export async function countNewListings(
 107:   admin: SupabaseClient,
 108:   since: string,
 109:   agentId: string,
 110: ): Promise<{ count: number; error: unknown }> {
 111:   if (!UUID_RE.test(agentId)) return { count: 0, error: new Error("invalid agent_id") };
 112:   const { count, error } = await admin
 113:     .from("listings")
 114:     .select("id", { count: "exact", head: true })
 115:     .gt("created_at", since)
 116:     .or(`owner_agent_id.is.null,owner_agent_id.eq.${agentId}`);
 117:   if (error) return { count: 0, error };
 118:   return { count: count ?? 0, error: null };
 119: }
 120:

  21: type Summary = {
  22:   sent: number;
  23:   failed: number;
  24:   removed: number;
  25:   skipped: "disabled" | "no_subscribers" | "nothing_new" | null;
  26: };

  28: function isAuthorized(header: string | null): boolean {
  29:   // Fail-closed: CRON_SECRET yoksa hiÃ§bir istek geÃ§mez (AC-S3).
  30:   const secret = process.env.CRON_SECRET;
  31:   if (!secret || !header) return false;
  32:   const expected = `Bearer ${secret}`;
  33:   if (header.length !== expected.length) return false;
  34:   return timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(header), Buffer.from(expected));
  35: }
  36: 
  37: function emptySummary(skipped: Summary["skipped"]): Summary {
  38:   return { sent: 0, failed: 0, removed: 0, skipped };
  39: }
  40: 
  41: async function mapLimit<T, R>(
  42:   items: T[],
  43:   limit: number,
  44:   fn: (item: T) => Promise<R>,
  45: ): Promise<R[]> {
  46:   const results: R[] = new Array(items.length);
  47:   let next = 0;
  48:   const workers = Array.from({ length: Math.min(limit, items.length) }, async () => {
  49:     while (next < items.length) {
  50:       const index = next++;
  51:       results[index] = await fn(items[index]);
  52:     }
  53:   });
  54:   await Promise.all(workers);
  55:   return results;
  56: }
  57: 
  58: type Outcome = "sent" | "failed" | "removed";
  59: 
  60: function statusOf(err: unknown): number | undefined {
  61:   return (err as { statusCode?: number } | null)?.statusCode;
  62: }
  63: 
  64: export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  65:   if (!isAuthorized(request.headers.get("authorization"))) {
  66:     return NextResponse.json({ error: "unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  67:   }

  72:     return NextResponse.json(emptySummary("disabled"));
  73:   }
  74:   webpush.setVapidDetails(vapid.subject, vapid.publicKey, vapid.privateKey);
  75: 
  76:   const admin = createServiceRoleClient();
  77:   // Pencere iÅŸaretÃ§isi: bu Ã§alÄ±ÅŸmanÄ±n baÅŸlangÄ±cÄ±. GÃ¶nderilen satÄ±rlarÄ±n last_notified_at'i bu deÄŸer olur.
  78:   const runAt = new Date().toISOString();
  79: 
  80:   const loaded = await loadTargets(admin);
  81:   if (loaded.error || !loaded.data) {
  82:     console.error("[push] cron db_failed: loadTargets", loaded.error);
  83:     return NextResponse.json({ error: "db_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  84:   }
  85:   const targets: PushTarget[] = loaded.data;
  86:   if (targets.length === 0) {
  87:     return NextResponse.json(emptySummary("no_subscribers"));
  88:   }
  89: 
  90:   // Ã–nce tÃ¼m sayÄ±mlar; herhangi biri hata verirse hiÃ§bir cihaza gÃ¶nderim yapÄ±lmaz.
  91:   const counts = await mapLimit(targets, CONCURRENCY, (t) => countNewListings(admin, t.since, t.agent_id));
  92:   const countFailure = counts.find((c) => c.error);
  93:   if (countFailure) {
  94:     console.error("[push] cron db_failed: countNewListings", countFailure.error);
  95:     return NextResponse.json({ error: "db_failed" }, { status: 500 });
  96:   }
  97: 
  98:   const pending = targets
  99:     .map((target, i) => ({ target, count: counts[i].count }))
 100:     .filter((p) => p.count > 0);
 101:   if (pending.length === 0) {
 102:     return NextResponse.json(emptySummary("nothing_new"));
 103:   }
 104: 
 105:   const outcomes = await mapLimit(pending, CONCURRENCY, async ({ target, count }): Promise<Outcome> => {
 106:     // Ä°kinci katman (SSRF): DB CHECK kÄ±sÄ±tÄ±na ek olarak gÃ¶ndermeden Ã¶nce yeniden doÄŸrula.

 110:       const { error } = await removeSubscription(admin, target.id);
 111:       if (error) console.error("[push] removeSubscription failed", error);

 113:     }

 132:     }

 136:     return "sent";
 137:   });
 138: 
 139:   const summary: Summary = {
 140:     sent: outcomes.filter((o) => o === "sent").length,

 142:     removed: outcomes.filter((o) => o === "removed").length,
 143:     skipped: null,
 144:   };
 145:   console.info("[push] cron", summary);
 146:   return NextResponse.json(summary);

 483:   // Dinleyici oturum soketine en fazla bir kez baÄŸlanÄ±r (yeniden baÄŸlanmada yeni soket iÃ§in tekrar).
 484:   _attachMessages(s, sock) {

 490:   async _onMessages(s, sock, u) {
 491:     if (!this._isCurrent(s, sock)) return;
 492:     const { messages, type } = u || {};
 493:     if (type !== 'notify' || !Array.isArray(messages)) return;
 494:     for (const msg of messages) {

 496:     }

 499:   async _onMessage(s, msg) {
 500:     // Kendi gÃ¶nderdiklerimiz ve metinsiz (tepki, protokol) mesajlar ilan adayÄ± deÄŸildir.
 501:     if (!msg || !msg.key || msg.key.fromMe) return;
 502:     const text = messageText(msg.message);
 503:     if (!text) return;
 504: 
 505:     const remoteJid = msg.key.remoteJid;
 506:     const selection = await this._selectionOf(s);
 507:     const outcome = classifyIncoming({ remoteJid }, selection);
 508:     bump(this.msgCounters, outcome);
 509:     if (outcome !== 'process') return;
 510: 
 511:     const groupName = (s.groupNames && s.groupNames.get(remoteJid)) || null;
 512:     const payload = { id: msg.key.id, text, group: remoteJid, groupName, agentId: s.agentId };
 513:     const ok = await this._postWebhook(payload);
 514:     if (!ok) bump(this.msgCounters, 'webhook_post_failed');
 515:   }

 517:   // Ortak kÃ¶prÃ¼nÃ¼n (bridge.js) webhook desenine uyumlu: 3 deneme, bekleme yok. Ä°Ã§erik loglanmaz.
 518:   async _postWebhook(payload) {
 519:     const { url, secret, fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch } = this.webhook || {};
 520:     if (!url || !secret) return false;
 521:     for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= WEBHOOK_MAX_ATTEMPTS; attempt += 1) {
 522:       try {
 523:         const res = await fetchImpl(url, {
 524:           method: 'POST',
 525:           headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${secret}` },
 526:           body: JSON.stringify(payload),
 527:         });
 528:         if (res.ok) return true;
 529:         logError('webhook yanÄ±tÄ± baÅŸarÄ±sÄ±z', { status: res.status });
 530:       } catch (e) {
 531:         logError('webhook baÄŸlantÄ± hatasÄ±', { error: (e && e.name) || 'error' });
 532:       }
 533:     }
 534:     return false;
 535:   }

 537:   async _onClose(s, sock, code) {
 538:     if (code === CONNECTION_REPLACED) {
 539:       // BaÅŸka yerde aÃ§Ä±ldÄ±: otomatik yeniden baÄŸlanma YOK (AC-8).
 540:       return this._terminate(
 541:         s,
 542:         { status: 'disconnected', error_code: 'replaced', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
 543:         { logout: false, deleteAuth: false },
 544:       );
 545:     }
 546:     if (code === LOGGED_OUT) {
 547:       // Telefondan Ã§Ä±karÄ±ldÄ± (AC-5): auth silinir, yeniden baÄŸlanma yok.
 548:       return this._terminate(
 549:         s,
 550:         { status: 'logged_out', error_code: null, qr: null, qr_expires_at: null, phone_masked: null },
 551:         { logout: false, deleteAuth: true },
 552:       );
 553:     }
 554:     // GeÃ§ici kopma: Ã¼stel backoff ile yeniden aÃ§; auth korunur.
 555:     const delay = backoffDelay(s.retries, this.rnd);
 556:     s.retries += 1;
 557:     s.sock = null;
 558:     this.counters.reconnects += 1;
 559:     log('baÄŸlantÄ± koptu, yeniden denenecek', { agent_id: s.agentId, status_code: code, retry_in_ms: delay });
 560:     this._schedule(() => this._guard('yeniden baÄŸlanma', () => this._reopen(s)), delay);
 561:   }

 566:   }

 568:   async _onCreds(s, sock, saveCreds) {

 584:   async _sweepNoQr() {
 585:     const nowMs = this.now();
 586:     for (const s of [...this.sessions.values()]) {

 594:     }
 595:   }

 601:   async _checkSubscriptions() {
 602:     for (const s of [...this.sessions.values()]) {
 603:       let active;
 604:       try {
 605:         active = await this.rest.isSubscriptionActive(s.agentId);
 606:       } catch (e) {

 611:         await this._terminate(

 613:           { status: 'disconnected', error_code: 'subscription_inactive', qr: null, qr_expires_at: null },
 614:           { logout: true, deleteAuth: true },

 624:   async _terminate(s, fields, { logout = false, deleteAuth = false } = {}) {
 625:     // Ä°dempotent: aynÄ± oturum iÃ§in ikinci sonlandÄ±rma (Ã¶r. iki sÃ¼pÃ¼rmenin aynÄ± anda

 630:     const sock = s.sock;
 631:     s.sock = null;
 632:     if (logout && sock) closeSocket(sock);
 633:     if (deleteAuth) removeAgentAuth(this.authRoot, s.agentId);
 634: 
 635:     this.counters.terminated += 1;

 637:     s.status = fields.status;

 642:       requested_action: null,
 643:       last_seen_at: this._iso(this.now()),
 644:     });

 646:       attempt_id: s.attemptId,
 647:       from_status: from,
 648:       to_status: fields.status,
 649:       error_code: fields.error_code || null,

-- 3) Bildirim: agent "son gÃ¶rme" zamanÄ±. Null ise sayaÃ§ son 7 gÃ¼ne bakar.
alter table agents add column if not exists listings_seen_at timestamptz;

-- YalnÄ±zca Ã§aÄŸÄ±ranÄ±n kendi satÄ±rÄ±ndaki listings_seen_at'Ä± yazar.
-- Hedef id istemciden alÄ±nmaz; agents_self_update policy'si baÅŸka satÄ±rÄ± engeller.
-- DÃ¶nÃ¼ÅŸ: etkilenen satÄ±r sayÄ±sÄ± (0 ise yazma yapÄ±lmadÄ±).
create or replace function mark_listings_seen() returns integer
language plpgsql
security invoker
set search_path = public
as $$
declare
    v_count integer;
begin
    update agents set listings_seen_at = now() where id = auth.uid();
    get diagnostics v_count = row_count;
    return v_count;
end;
$$;

grant execute on function mark_listings_seen() to authenticated;

   8: export async function approveListing(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
   9:   const rawListingId = String(formData.get("raw_listing_id") ?? "");
  10:   if (!rawListingId) return;
  11: 
  12:   // Uygulama seviyesi admin kontrolÃ¼ â€” RPC'den Ã–NCE. RLS/SECURITY INVOKER
  13:   // ikinci katman olarak kalÄ±r (defense-in-depth).
  14:   const auth = await requireAdmin();
  15:   if (!auth.ok) {
  16:     if (auth.reason === "no-session") {
  17:       redirect("/login");
  18:     }
  19:     throw new Error("Bu iÅŸlem iÃ§in yetkiniz yok");
  20:   }
  21:   const { supabase } = auth;
  22: 
  23:   const { error } = await supabase.rpc("approve_raw_listing", {
  24:     p_raw_listing_id: rawListingId,
  25:   });
  26: 
  27:   if (error) {
  28:     console.error("Ä°lan onaylanamadÄ±", error);
  29:     throw new Error("Ä°lan onaylanamadÄ±, lÃ¼tfen tekrar deneyin");
  30:   }
  31: 
  32:   revalidatePath("/admin/listings");
  33: }
  34: 
  35: // EML-107 AC-7 / AC-S5: eÅŸik yalnÄ±z admin RPC'siyle deÄŸiÅŸir. Yetki kontrolÃ¼ RPC'den Ã¶nce;
  36: // RPC de kendi iÃ§inde admin doÄŸrular (defense-in-depth).
  37: export async function setPersonalThreshold(
  38:   value: number,
  39: ): Promise<{ ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string }> {
  40:   if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0 || value > 1) {
  41:     return { ok: false, error: "invalid_value" };
  42:   }
  43: 
  44:   const auth = await requireAdmin();
  45:   if (!auth.ok) {
  46:     if (auth.reason === "no-session") {
  47:       redirect("/login");
  48:     }
  49:     return { ok: false, error: "forbidden" };
  50:   }
  51:   const { supabase } = auth;
  52: 
  53:   const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_set_threshold", { p_threshold: value });
  54:   if (error) {
  55:     console.error("eÅŸik gÃ¼ncellenemedi", error);
  56:     return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  57:   }
  58:   const result = (data ?? {}) as { ok?: boolean; error?: string };
  59:   if (result.ok === true) {
  60:     revalidatePath("/admin/listings");
  61:     return { ok: true };
  62:   }
  63:   return { ok: false, error: typeof result.error === "string" ? result.error : "rpc_failed" };
  64: }
  65: 
  66: export async function rejectListing(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  67:   const rawListingId = String(formData.get("raw_listing_id") ?? "");
  68:   if (!rawListingId) return;
  69: 
  70:   const auth = await requireAdmin();
  71:   if (!auth.ok) {
  72:     if (auth.reason === "no-session") {
  73:       redirect("/login");
  74:     }
  75:     throw new Error("Bu iÅŸlem iÃ§in yetkiniz yok");
  76:   }
  77:   const { supabase } = auth;
  78: 
  79:   const { error } = await supabase.rpc("reject_raw_listing", {
  80:     p_raw_listing_id: rawListingId,
  81:   });
  82: 
  83:   if (error) {
  84:     console.error("Ä°lan reddedilemedi", error);
  85:     throw new Error("Ä°lan reddedilemedi, lÃ¼tfen tekrar deneyin");
  86:   }
  87:

  89: }

   1: import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";

   8: 
   9: const THRESHOLD_KEY = "personal_auto_approve_threshold";
  10: const DEFAULT_THRESHOLD = 0.7;
  11: 
  12: // EML-107 AC-7: mevcut eÅŸik. Okunamazsa varsayÄ±lan gÃ¶sterilir ve bu ayrÄ±ca belirtilir.
  13: async function readThreshold(
  14:   supabase: SupabaseClient,
  15: ): Promise<{ value: number; failed: boolean }> {
  16:   try {
  17:     const { data, error } = await supabase
  18:       .from("app_settings")
  19:       .select("value")
  20:       .eq("key", THRESHOLD_KEY)
  21:       .maybeSingle();
  22:     if (error) return { value: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, failed: true };
  23:     const value = (data as { value?: unknown } | null)?.value;
  24:     return typeof value === "number"
  25:       ? { value, failed: false }
  26:       : { value: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, failed: false };
  27:   } catch {
  28:     return { value: DEFAULT_THRESHOLD, failed: true };
  29:   }
  30: }

  54: };

  71:

  82:

===== web/src/app/admin/listings/threshold-form.tsx =====
   1: "use client";
   2: 
   3: import { type FormEvent, useState } from "react";
   4: 
   5: import { Alert } from "@/components/ui/alert";
   6: 
   7: // EML-107 AC-7 (eÅŸik UI) ve AC-S5. EÅŸik app_settings('personal_auto_approve_threshold'); 0-1 dÄ±ÅŸÄ± reddedilir.
   8: // Kaydetme onSave prop'undan (server action â†’ admin_set_threshold RPC) gelir.
   9: 
  10: const RANGE_ERROR = "0 ile 1 arasÄ±nda bir deÄŸer girin";
  11: const SAVE_ERROR_MESSAGES: Partial<Record<string, string>> = {
  12:   forbidden: "Yetkiniz yok",
  13:   invalid_value: RANGE_ERROR,
  14: };
  15: const DEFAULT_SAVE_ERROR = "Kaydedilemedi, tekrar deneyin.";
  16: 
  17: export type ThresholdSaveResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string };
  18: 
  19: type Notice = { kind: "ok" | "error"; text: string } | null;
  20: 
  21: // BoÅŸ, sayÄ± olmayan, sonsuz ve 0-1 dÄ±ÅŸÄ± deÄŸerler geÃ§ersiz.
  22: function parseThreshold(input: string): number | null {
  23:   if (input.trim() === "") return null;
  24:   const n = Number(input);
  25:   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 1 ? n : null;
  26: }
  27: 
  28: export function ThresholdForm({
  29:   value,
  30:   onSave,
  31: }: {
  32:   value: number;
  33:   onSave: (value: number) => Promise<ThresholdSaveResult>;
  34: }) {
  35:   const [input, setInput] = useState(String(value));
  36:   const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  37:   const [notice, setNotice] = useState<Notice>(null);
  38: 
  39:   async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
  40:     event.preventDefault();
  41:     const parsed = parseThreshold(input);
  42:     if (parsed === null) {
  43:       setNotice({ kind: "error", text: RANGE_ERROR });
  44:       return;
  45:     }
  46:     setBusy(true);
  47:     setNotice(null);
  48:     const res = await onSave(parsed);
  49:     setBusy(false);
  50:     if (res.ok) {
  51:       setNotice({ kind: "ok", text: "Kaydedildi" });
  52:       return;
  53:     }
  54:     setNotice({ kind: "error", text: SAVE_ERROR_MESSAGES[res.error] ?? DEFAULT_SAVE_ERROR });
  55:   }
  56: 
  57:   return (
  58:     <form noValidate onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-4 space-y-3">
  59:       <label className="block text-sm text-zinc-700">
  60:         Otomatik onay eÅŸiÄŸi (0-1)
  61:         <input
  62:           type="number"
  63:           min={0}
  64:           max={1}
  65:           step="0.01"
  66:           inputMode="decimal"
  67:           value={input}
  68:           onChange={(event) => setInput(event.target.value)}
  69:           className="mt-1 block w-full max-w-xs rounded border px-3 py-1.5 text-sm"
  70:         />
  71:       </label>
  72:       <button
  73:         type="submit"
  74:         disabled={busy}
  75:         className="rounded bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-white hover:bg-zinc-700 disabled:opacity-50"
  76:       >
  77:         Kaydet
  78:       </button>
  79:       {notice && (
  80:         <Alert variant={notice.kind === "ok" ? "success" : "error"}>
  81:           <p>{notice.text}</p>
  82:         </Alert>
  83:       )}
  84:     </form>
  85:   );
  86: }

===== web/src/lib/whatsapp/groups.ts =====
   1: import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
   2: 
   3: // EML-107 DavranÄ±ÅŸ SÃ¶zleÅŸmesi: get_my_wa_groups / set_my_wa_group_selection.
   4: // RPC sarmalayÄ±cÄ±larÄ± agent_id ALMAZ (AC-S1/AC-S2): kimlik her zaman auth.uid() ile.
   5: 
   6: export type WaGroup = { jid: string; name: string | null; selected: boolean };
   7: 
   8: export type WaGroupsOk = {
   9:   ok: true;
  10:   status: string;
  11:   groups: WaGroup[];
  12:   max: number;
  13:   groups_synced_at: string | null;
  14: };
  15: 
  16: export type WaGroupsResult = WaGroupsOk | { ok: false; error: string };
  17: 
  18: export type WaSetGroupsResult = { ok: true; selected: number } | { ok: false; error: string };
  19: 
  20: type RawRecord = Record<string, unknown>;
  21: 
  22: function asRecord(raw: unknown): RawRecord {
  23:   return raw !== null && typeof raw === "object" ? (raw as RawRecord) : {};
  24: }
  25: 
  26: function parseGroups(raw: unknown): WaGroup[] {
  27:   if (!Array.isArray(raw)) return [];
  28:   const groups: WaGroup[] = [];
  29:   for (const item of raw) {
  30:     const r = asRecord(item);
  31:     if (typeof r.jid !== "string") continue;
  32:     groups.push({
  33:       jid: r.jid,
  34:       name: typeof r.name === "string" ? r.name : null,
  35:       selected: r.selected === true,
  36:     });
  37:   }
  38:   return groups;
  39: }
  40: 
  41: /** Kendi WhatsApp gruplarÄ±m (get_my_wa_groups). */
  42: export async function getMyWaGroups(supabase: SupabaseClient): Promise<WaGroupsResult> {
  43:   try {
  44:     const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("get_my_wa_groups");
  45:     if (error) return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  46:     const r = asRecord(data);
  47:     if (r.ok !== true) {
  48:       return { ok: false, error: typeof r.error === "string" ? r.error : "rpc_failed" };
  49:     }
  50:     return {
  51:       ok: true,
  52:       status: typeof r.status === "string" ? r.status : "none",
  53:       groups: parseGroups(r.groups),
  54:       max: typeof r.max === "number" ? r.max : 20,
  55:       groups_synced_at: typeof r.groups_synced_at === "string" ? r.groups_synced_at : null,
  56:     };
  57:   } catch {
  58:     return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  59:   }
  60: }
  61: 
  62: /** Okunacak grup seÃ§imini tamamen deÄŸiÅŸtirir (set_my_wa_group_selection). */
  63: export async function setMyWaGroupSelection(
  64:   supabase: SupabaseClient,
  65:   jids: string[],
  66: ): Promise<WaSetGroupsResult> {
  67:   try {
  68:     const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc("set_my_wa_group_selection", {
  69:       p_group_jids: jids,
  70:     });
  71:     if (error) return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  72:     const r = asRecord(data);
  73:     if (r.ok === true) {
  74:       return { ok: true, selected: typeof r.selected === "number" ? r.selected : 0 };
  75:     }
  76:     return { ok: false, error: typeof r.error === "string" ? r.error : "rpc_failed" };
  77:   } catch {
  78:     return { ok: false, error: "rpc_failed" };
  79:   }
  80: }

  14:   unauthorized: "Oturumunuz sona erdi, tekrar giriÅŸ yapÄ±n.",
