  25: export function GroupPicker({ initial, onSave }: GroupPickerProps) {
  26:   const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<string>>(
  27:     () => new Set(initial.groups.filter((g) => g.selected).map((g) => g.jid)),
  28:   );
  29:   const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  30:   const [notice, setNotice] = useState<Notice>(null);
  31:   // Kaydetmede not_connected dÃ¶nerse, sayfa yenilenene kadar salt-okunur kalÄ±r.
  32:   const [notConnected, setNotConnected] = useState(false);
  33: 
  34:   const readonly = initial.status !== "connected" || notConnected;
  35: 
  36:   if (initial.status === "none") {
  37:     return (
  38:       <section aria-labelledby="wa-groups-title" className="max-w-xl space-y-4">
  39:         <h2 id="wa-groups-title" className="text-lg font-semibold text-ink-900">
  40:           Grup seÃ§imi
  41:         </h2>
  42:         <p className="text-sm text-ink-900">{"Ã–nce WhatsApp'Ä±nÄ± baÄŸla"}</p>
  43:       </section>
  44:     );
  45:   }
  46: 
  47:   const awaitingSync = initial.groups.length === 0 && initial.groups_synced_at === null;
  48: 
  49:   function toggle(jid: string) {
  50:     setNotice(null);
  51:     setSelected((prev) => {
  52:       const next = new Set(prev);
  53:       if (next.has(jid)) next.delete(jid);
  54:       else if (next.size < initial.max) next.add(jid);
  55:       return next;
  56:     });
  57:   }
  58: 
  59:   async function handleSave() {
  60:     const jids = initial.groups.filter((g) => selected.has(g.jid)).map((g) => g.jid);
  61:     setBusy(true);
  62:     setNotice(null);
  63:     const res = await onSave(jids);
  64:     setBusy(false);
  65:     if (res.ok) {
  66:       setNotice(
  67:         res.selected === 0
  68:           ? { kind: "ok", text: "HiÃ§bir grup okunmuyor" }
  69:           : { kind: "ok", text: "Kaydedildi" },
  70:       );
  71:       return;
  72:     }
  73:     if (res.error === "not_connected") {
  74:       setNotConnected(true);
  75:       return;
  76:     }
  77:     setNotice({ kind: "error", text: SAVE_ERROR_MESSAGES[res.error] ?? DEFAULT_SAVE_ERROR });
  78:   }
  79: 
  80:   const primaryButton =
  81:     "rounded-md bg-terracotta-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:opacity-90 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-ink-900 disabled:opacity-50";
  82: 
  83:   return (
  84:     <section aria-labelledby="wa-groups-title" className="max-w-xl space-y-4">
  85:       <h2 id="wa-groups-title" className="text-lg font-semibold text-ink-900">
  86:         Grup seÃ§imi
  87:       </h2>
  88: 
  89:       {readonly && (
  90:         <Alert variant="error">

  92:             WhatsApp baÄŸlantÄ±nÄ±z aktif deÄŸil; grup seÃ§imi salt okunur. <strong>Yeniden baÄŸlan</strong>
  93:           </p>
  94:         </Alert>
  95:       )}
  96: 
  97:       {awaitingSync ? (
  98:         <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Gruplar yÃ¼kleniyorâ€¦</p>
  99:       ) : (
 100:         <>
 101:           <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">{`${selected.size}/${initial.max}`}</p>
 102:           <ul className="space-y-2">
 103:             {initial.groups.map((group) => {
 104:               const checked = selected.has(group.jid);
 105:               const full = !checked && selected.size >= initial.max;
 106:               return (
 107:                 <li key={group.jid}>
 108:                   <label className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-ink-900">
 109:                     <input
 110:                       type="checkbox"
 111:                       className="mt-1 h-4 w-4 shrink-0"
 112:                       checked={checked}
 113:                       disabled={readonly || busy || full}
 114:                       onChange={() => toggle(group.jid)}
 115:                     />
 116:                     <span className="min-w-0 break-words">{group.name ?? "(adsÄ±z grup)"}</span>
 117:                   </label>
 118:                 </li>
 119:               );
 120:             })}
 121:           </ul>
 122: 
 123:           {initial.groups.length > 0 && (
 124:             <button
 125:               type="button"
 126:               onClick={handleSave}
 127:               disabled={readonly || busy}
 128:               className={primaryButton}
 129:             >
 130:               Kaydet
 131:             </button>
 132:           )}
 133:         </>
 134:       )}
 135:

 137:         <Alert variant={notice.kind === "ok" ? "success" : "error"}>

 139:         </Alert>
 140:       )}
 141:     </section>
 142:   );

  36: };
  37: 
  38: const STATUS_ERROR_MESSAGES: Partial<Record<string, string>> = {

  40:   no_qr_generated: "BaÄŸlantÄ± baÅŸlatÄ±lamadÄ±, tekrar deneyin.",
  41:   capacity: "Åu an yeni baÄŸlantÄ± aÃ§Ä±lamÄ±yor.",

  43:   sidecar_misconfigured: "Servis ÅŸu an kullanÄ±lamÄ±yor.",
  44: };
  45: 
  46: const DISCONNECTED_MESSAGES: Partial<Record<string, string>> = {
  47:   qr_expired: "SÃ¼re doldu, tekrar deneyin.",
  48:   replaced: "BaÄŸlantÄ± baÅŸka bir yerde aÃ§Ä±ldÄ±.",
  49:   subscription_inactive: "AboneliÄŸiniz aktif deÄŸil.",
  50: };
  51: 
  52: // Panel yalnÄ±z yerel "kaldÄ±rÄ±lÄ±yor" ara durumunu ekler; diÄŸerleri sunucu ÅŸekli.
  53: type ViewStatus = WaStatusView | { status: "disconnecting" };
  54: 
  55: type RetryKind = "consent" | "connect" | "disconnect";
  56: type PanelError = { message: string; retry?: RetryKind } | null;
  57: 
  58: export type WaPanelProps = {
  59:   initialStatus: WaStatusView;
  60:   consentAccepted: boolean;
  61:   /** EML-107: grup listesi (sunucu, get_my_wa_groups). Yoksa grup bÃ¶lÃ¼mÃ¼ gÃ¶sterilmez. */
  62:   groups?: WaGroupsResult;
  63: };
  64: 
  65: /** Hata/durum mesajÄ± gereken durumlar; "BaÄŸla" yerine "Tekrar dene" gÃ¶sterilir. */
  66: function statusMessage(status: ViewStatus): string | null {
  67:   switch (status.status) {
  68:     case "logged_out":
  69:       return LOGGED_OUT_MESSAGE;
  70:     case "disconnected": {
  71:       const known = status.error_code ? DISCONNECTED_MESSAGES[status.error_code] : undefined;
  72:       return known ?? "BaÄŸlantÄ± kesildi, tekrar baÄŸlayabilirsiniz.";
  73:     }
  74:     case "error": {
  75:       const known = status.error_code ? STATUS_ERROR_MESSAGES[status.error_code] : undefined;
  76:       return known ?? DEFAULT_ERROR;
  77:     }
  78:     default:
  79:       return null;
  80:   }
  81: }
  82: 
  83: function actionErrorMessage(code: string): string {
  84:   return ACTION_ERROR_MESSAGES[code] ?? DEFAULT_ERROR;
  85: }
  86: 
  87: // KaldÄ±rma sÄ±rasÄ±nda sidecar henÃ¼z iÅŸlemediyse "connected" gÃ¶rÃ¼nÃ¼r; yalnÄ±z sonuÃ§ durumlarÄ± kabul edilir.
  88: const DISCONNECT_TERMINAL = new Set(["none", "disconnected", "error", "logged_out"]);
  89: 
  90: function applyPoll(prev: ViewStatus, res: WaStatusResult, timedOut: boolean): ViewStatus {
  91:   if (!res.ok) return { status: "error", error_code: null };
  92:   if (prev.status === "disconnecting") {
  93:     if (timedOut) return { status: "error", error_code: "sidecar_timeout" };
  94:     return DISCONNECT_TERMINAL.has(res.status) ? res : prev;
  95:   }
  96:   return res;
  97: }
  98: 
  99: export function WaPanel({ initialStatus, consentAccepted, groups }: WaPanelProps) {
 100:   const [status, setStatus] = useState<ViewStatus>(initialStatus);
 101:   const [consent, setConsent] = useState(consentAccepted);
 102:   const [ticked, setTicked] = useState(false);
 103:   const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
 104:   const [error, setError] = useState<PanelError>(null);
 105:   const [confirmOpen, setConfirmOpen] = useState(false);
 106:   const [qrImage, setQrImage] = useState<{ value: string; svg: string } | null>(null);
 107:   const pollCountRef = useRef(0);
 108: 
 109:   const isPolling =
 110:     status.status === "pending" || status.status === "qr" || status.status === "disconnecting";
 111: 
 112:   // 2 sn poll: yalnÄ±z bekleme/QR/kaldÄ±rma sÃ¼rerken.
 113:   useEffect(() => {
 114:     if (!isPolling) return;
 115:     const id = setInterval(async () => {
 116:       pollCountRef.current += 1;
 117:       const timedOut = pollCountRef.current > DISCONNECT_MAX_POLLS;
 118:       const res = await getMyWaStatus();
 119:       setStatus((prev) => applyPoll(prev, res, timedOut));
 120:     }, POLL_MS);
 121:     return () => clearInterval(id);
 122:   }, [isPolling]);
 123: 
 124:   const qrValue = status.status === "qr" ? status.qr : null;
 125:

 127:   useEffect(() => {
 128:     if (qrValue === null) return;
 129:     let cancelled = false;

 131:       .then((svg) => {
 132:         if (!cancelled) setQrImage({ value: qrValue, svg });
 133:       })
 134:       .catch(() => {
 135:         if (!cancelled) setError({ message: "QR kodu gÃ¶sterilemedi, tekrar deneyin.", retry: "connect" });
 136:       });
 137:     return () => {
 138:       cancelled = true;
 139:     };
 140:   }, [qrValue]);
 141: 
 142:   async function handleAccept() {
 143:     if (!ticked) return;
 144:     setBusy(true);
 145:     setError(null);
 146:     const res = await acceptWaConsent(WA_CONSENT_VERSION);
 147:     setBusy(false);

 149:       setConsent(true);

 151:       return;

 153:     setError({ message: "Onay kaydedilemedi, tekrar deneyin.", retry: "consent" });
 154:   }

 156:   async function handleConnect() {

 158:     setError(null);

 162:       if (res.error === "consent_required") {

 175:

 181:     setBusy(false);

  17: // Next.js 16 Cache Components: cookies() (Supabase session) her istekte
  18: // deÄŸiÅŸebilir, bu route segment'i prerender edilemez â€” statik build'i
  19: // engellemesin diye bloklu/dinamik render'a izin veriyoruz.
  20: export const instant = false;

  27: // Hata ekranÄ±ndaki "Tekrar dene" â€” mevcut filtre/sayfa parametreleri korunur.
  28: function buildRetryHref(params: SearchParams): string {
  29:   const query = new URLSearchParams();
  30:   for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(params)) {
  31:     if (Array.isArray(value)) {
  32:       for (const item of value) query.append(key, item);
  33:     } else if (value !== undefined) {
  34:       query.append(key, value);
  35:     }
  36:   }
  37:   const qs = query.toString();
  38:   return qs ? `/agent/dashboard?${qs}` : "/agent/dashboard";
  39: }
  40: 
  41: const BADGE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  42:   trialing: "Deneme sÃ¼rÃ¼mÃ¼",
  43:   active: "Aktif",
  44:   past_due: "Ã–deme gecikmiÅŸ",
  45:   canceled: "Ä°ptal edildi",
  46: };
  47: 
  48: function badgeLabel(status: string | null): string {
  49:   if (!status) return "AboneliÄŸiniz yok";
  50:   return BADGE_LABELS[status] ?? `Abonelik: ${status}`;
  51: }
  52: 
  53: // YalnÄ±z hasAccess sonrasÄ± Ã§aÄŸrÄ±lÄ±r. SeÃ§enekler, ilanlar ve sayaÃ§ sÄ±rayla
  54: // awaited edilir (aynÄ± Supabase builder'Ä± Ã¼zerinde Ã§akÄ±ÅŸmasÄ±n diye).
  55: async function loadListings(
  56:   supabase: SupabaseClient,
  57:   params: SearchParams,
  58:   userId: string,
  59: ) {
  60:   const rawIl = typeof params.il === "string" ? params.il : null;
  61:   const options = await fetchFilterOptions(supabase, rawIl);
  62:   const { filters, hadInvalid } = parseFeedFilters(params, options);
  63:   const feed = await fetchFeed(supabase, filters);
  64: 
  65:   const { data: agent } = await supabase
  66:     .from("agents")
  67:     .select("listings_seen_at")
  68:     .eq("id", userId)
  69:     .maybeSingle();
  70:   const seenAt =
  71:     (agent as { listings_seen_at: string | null } | null)?.listings_seen_at ?? null;
  72:   const unread = await getUnreadCount(supabase, userId, seenAt);
  73: 
  74:   return { options, filters, hadInvalid, feed, unread };
  75: }

  77: export default async function AgentDashboardPage({
  78:   searchParams,
  79: }: {
  80:   searchParams: Promise<SearchParams>;
  81: }) {

===== web/src/lib/listings-feed/filters.ts =====
   1: export const ROOM_OPTIONS = ["1+0", "1+1", "2+1", "3+1", "4+1", "5+"] as const;
   2: 
   3: export type FeedFilterOptions = {
   4:   cities: string[];
   5:   districts: string[];
   6:   groups: string[];
   7: };
   8: 
   9: export type ParsedFilters = {
  10:   grup: string | null;
  11:   fiyatMin: number | null;
  12:   fiyatMax: number | null;
  13:   oda: string | null;
  14:   il: string | null;
  15:   ilce: string | null;
  16:   /** EML-107 AC-8: "mine" yalnÄ±z kendi gruplarÄ±mdaki kiÅŸisel ilanlarÄ± getirir; null = tÃ¼mÃ¼. */
  17:   kapsam: "mine" | null;
  18:   sayfa: number;
  19: };
  20: 
  21: export type SearchParams = Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>;
  22: 
  23: const MAX_PRICE = 1_000_000_000_000;
  24: const MAX_TEXT_LENGTH = 100;
  25: const MAX_PAGE = 1000;
  26: 
  27: type Picked<T> = { value: T | null; invalid: boolean };
  28: 
  29: // Parametre yoksa geÃ§ersiz sayÄ±lmaz; varsa ama parse edilemiyorsa geÃ§ersizdir.
  30: function pick<T>(
  31:   raw: string | string[] | undefined,
  32:   parse: (value: string) => T | null,
  33: ): Picked<T> {
  34:   if (raw === undefined) return { value: null, invalid: false };
  35:   const value = typeof raw === "string" ? parse(raw) : null;
  36:   return { value, invalid: value === null };
  37: }
  38: 
  39: function parsePrice(value: string): number | null {
  40:   if (!/^\d+$/.test(value)) return null;
  41:   const amount = Number(value);
  42:   return amount <= MAX_PRICE ? amount : null;
  43: }
  44: 
  45: function parseScope(value: string): "mine" | null {
  46:   return value === "mine" ? "mine" : null;
  47: }
  48: 
  49: function parseRoom(value: string): string | null {
  50:   return (ROOM_OPTIONS as readonly string[]).includes(value) ? value : null;
  51: }
  52: 
  53: function allowOnly(allowed: string[]) {
  54:   return (value: string): string | null =>
  55:     value.length <= MAX_TEXT_LENGTH && allowed.includes(value) ? value : null;
  56: }
  57: 
  58: function parsePage(raw: string | string[] | undefined): number {
  59:   if (typeof raw !== "string") return 1;
  60:   const page = Number.parseInt(raw, 10);
  61:   if (!Number.isFinite(page)) return 1;
  62:   return Math.min(MAX_PAGE, Math.max(1, page));
  63: }
  64: 
  65: export function parseFeedFilters(
  66:   params: SearchParams,
  67:   options: FeedFilterOptions,
  68: ): { filters: ParsedFilters; hadInvalid: boolean } {
  69:   const min = pick(params.fiyat_min, parsePrice);
  70:   const max = pick(params.fiyat_max, parsePrice);
  71:   const oda = pick(params.oda, parseRoom);
  72:   const il = pick(params.il, allowOnly(options.cities));
  73:   const ilce = pick(params.ilce, allowOnly(options.districts));
  74:   const grup = pick(params.grup, allowOnly(options.groups));
  75:   const kapsam = pick(params.kapsam, parseScope);
  76: 
  77:   let fiyatMin = min.value;
  78:   let fiyatMax = max.value;
  79:   if (fiyatMin !== null && fiyatMax !== null && fiyatMin > fiyatMax) {
  80:     [fiyatMin, fiyatMax] = [fiyatMax, fiyatMin];
  81:   }
  82: 
  83:   const hadInvalid = [min, max, oda, il, ilce, grup, kapsam].some((p) => p.invalid);
  84: 
  85:   return {
  86:     filters: {
  87:       grup: grup.value,
  88:       fiyatMin,
  89:       fiyatMax,
  90:       oda: oda.value,
  91:       il: il.value,
  92:       ilce: ilce.value,
  93:       kapsam: kapsam.value,
  94:       sayfa: parsePage(params.sayfa),
  95:     },
  96:     hadInvalid,
  97:   };
  98: }
===== web/src/lib/listings-feed/query.ts =====
   1: import type { SupabaseClient } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
   2: 
   3: import type { FeedFilterOptions, ParsedFilters } from "./filters";
   4: 
   5: export const QUERY_TIMEOUT_MS = 5_000;
   6: 
   7: // AÃ§Ä±k satÄ±r: sunucu tÃ¼m ilan alanlarÄ±nÄ± dÃ¶ner (EML-98 AC-1).
   8: export type FeedRow = {
   9:   id: string;
  10:   title: string;
  11:   price: number | null;
  12:   currency: string;
  13:   rooms: string | null;
  14:   area_m2: number | null;
  15:   city: string | null;
  16:   district: string | null;
  17:   source_group_name: string | null;
  18:   created_at: string;
  19:   /** EML-107 AC-8: dolu ise kiÅŸisel ilan (kendi grubum) â€” rozet iÃ§in. */
  20:   owner_agent_id?: string | null;
  21: };
  22: 
  23: export type OpenFeedRow = FeedRow & { locked: false };
  24: 
  25: // Kilitli satÄ±r: yalnÄ±z bu 5 anahtar (AC-4 / AC-S2). BaÅŸka alan gelmez.
  26: export type LockedFeedRow = {
  27:   id: string;
  28:   city: string | null;
  29:   district: string | null;
  30:   created_at: string;
  31:   locked: true;
  32: };
  33: 
  34: export type FeedItem = OpenFeedRow | LockedFeedRow;
  35: 
  36: export type FeedResult =
  37:   | {
  38:       ok: true;
  39:       items: FeedItem[];
  40:       lockedCount: number | null;
  41:       limit: number | null;
  42:       openToday: number;
  43:       reason?: string;
  44:     }
  45:   | { ok: false; code: string };
  46: 
  47: type FeedRpcBody = {
  48:   items?: FeedItem[];
  49:   locked_count?: number;
  50:   limit?: number | null;
  51:   open_today?: number;
  52:   reason?: string;
  53: };
  54: 
  55: export async function fetchFeed(
  56:   supabase: SupabaseClient,
  57:   filters: ParsedFilters,
  58: ): Promise<FeedResult> {
  59:   try {
  60:     const { data, error } = await supabase
  61:       .rpc("feed_listings", {
  62:         p_filters: {
  63:           il: filters.il,
  64:           ilce: filters.ilce,
  65:           grup: filters.grup,
  66:           fiyatMin: filters.fiyatMin,
  67:           fiyatMax: filters.fiyatMax,
  68:           oda: filters.oda,
  69:           // scope yalnÄ±z "mine" iken gider; varsayÄ±lan Ã§aÄŸrÄ± ÅŸekli deÄŸiÅŸmez.
  70:           ...(filters.kapsam === "mine" ? { scope: "mine" } : {}),
  71:         },
  72:         p_page: filters.sayfa,
  73:       })
  74:       .abortSignal(AbortSignal.timeout(QUERY_TIMEOUT_MS));
  75: 
  76:     if (error) {
  77:       console.error("[dashboard] feed rpc failed", { code: error.code });
  78:       return { ok: false, code: error.code ?? "unknown" };
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     const body = (data ?? {}) as FeedRpcBody;
  82:     const result: FeedResult = {
  83:       ok: true,
  84:       items: body.items ?? [],
  85:       lockedCount: body.locked_count ?? null,
  86:       limit: body.limit ?? null,
  87:       openToday: body.open_today ?? 0,
  88:     };
  89:     if (body.reason !== undefined) result.reason = body.reason;
  90:     return result;
  91:   } catch (err) {
  92:     const code = err instanceof Error ? err.name : "unknown";
  93:     console.error("[dashboard] feed rpc failed", { code });
  94:     return { ok: false, code };
  95:   }
  96: }
  97: 
  98: export async function fetchFilterOptions(
  99:   supabase: SupabaseClient,
 100:   il: string | null,
 101: ): Promise<FeedFilterOptions> {
 102:   const empty: FeedFilterOptions = { cities: [], districts: [], groups: [] };
 103: 
 104:   try {
 105:     const { data, error } = await supabase
 106:       .rpc("feed_filter_options", { p_il: il ?? null })
 107:       .abortSignal(AbortSignal.timeout(QUERY_TIMEOUT_MS));
 108: 
 109:     if (error) {
 110:       console.error("[dashboard] feed options rpc failed", { code: error.code });
 111:       return empty;
 112:     }
 113: 
 114:     const body = (data ?? {}) as Partial<FeedFilterOptions>;
 115:     return {
 116:       cities: body.cities ?? [],
 117:       districts: body.districts ?? [],
 118:       groups: body.groups ?? [],
 119:     };
 120:   } catch (err) {
 121:     const code = err instanceof Error ? err.name : "unknown";
 122:     console.error("[dashboard] feed options rpc failed", { code });
 123:     return empty;
 124:   }
 125: }
